### Now, the Combination for a Female Ascetic is Described

-> If a malefic planet is in the seventh house and there are planets in the ninth house, she certainly attains <term>pravrajya</term> <note>renunciation or the monastic life</note>; the type of renunciation depends on the planet in the ninth house. <-

**Meaning—** If at the time of a woman's birth, a malefic planet <note>such as Mars or Saturn</note> is situated in the seventh house, and there are definitely planets in the ninth house, then that woman takes up the life of a mendicant. However, she adopts the specific type of renunciation corresponding to the planet situated in the ninth house. For example:
* If the **Sun** is strong, she becomes a female ascetic who performs penance.
* From the **Moon**, she becomes a *Kapalini* <note>a follower of the Shaiva path, often carrying a skull-bowl</note>.
* From **Mars**, she wears ochre-colored robes <note>a traditional sannyasini</note>.
* From **Mercury**, she becomes a *Dandini* <note>one who carries a ritual staff</note>.
* From **Jupiter**, she becomes a *Kapalini* <note>here likely referring to a high-status religious practitioner</note>.
* From **Venus**, she becomes a *Chakradharini* <note>one who holds a discus or follows a specific tantric path</note>.
* From **Saturn**, she remains naked <note>referring to the Digambara or similar ascetic traditions</note>.

Such combinations must certainly be examined before marriage, at the time of matching birth charts, or when selecting a groom—that is, before the "giving away of the maiden" <note>Kanyadana</note>. (34) And so says <term>Varaha</term> <note>Varahamihira</note>: "With a malefic in the seventh, a young woman undoubtedly attains the renunciation corresponding to the planet in the ninth house. All this should be considered during the marriage, the selection ceremony, and the time of giving the bride." Furthermore, in another text: "If a malefic is in the seventh, the <term>pravrajya</term> <note>renunciation</note> mentioned for that planet occurs; there is no doubt regarding the woman's husband. Astrologers should logically consider all this during the gift of the bride, the marriage, and at the time of inquiry." (1) The combinations for renunciation at the time of birth have been mentioned previously; those for women...

### Now, the combination for a scholar of the scriptures

->**When Mercury, Jupiter, and Venus, along with the Moon, are strong and situated in the sign of the Moon <note>Cancer</note> at the time of birth,**<-
->**the woman becomes a speaker of divine truths and skilled in many scriptures. (35)**<-

Meaning—If at the time of a woman's birth, Mercury, Jupiter, Venus, and the Moon are positioned with strength in the rising sign <note>Lagna</note> within the zodiac sign of Cancer, then that woman becomes famous as a "Brahmavadini" <note>a female philosopher or speaker of sacred knowledge</note> and is proficient in all scriptures. ### Now, the combination for a "Poison Maiden" from the Yavana Jataka <note>The "Greek Nativity," an ancient astrological text</note>

->**On the Bhadra lunar days, when Ashlesha, Shatabhisha, or Krittika constellations occur,**<-
->**on the days of Saturn, Mars, or the Sun, a Poison Maiden is born. (36)**<-

Meaning—The first combination: a girl born on the 2nd lunar day <note>Dwitiya</note> in the Ashlesha constellation on a Saturday. The second combination: the 7th lunar day <note>Saptami</note> in the Shatabhisha constellation on a Tuesday. The third combination: the 12th lunar day <note>Dwadashi</note> in the Krittika constellation on a Sunday. A girl born in such combinations is called a "Poison Maiden" <note>original: "Visha Kanya"</note>; she brings destruction to her husband. (36) And so it is said: "The 12th lunar day with Shatabhisha on a Sunday; the 7th with Vishakha on a Tuesday; the 2nd with Ashlesha on a Saturday—thus is a Poison Maiden born." Also in the *Jatakalankara*: "She who takes birth in the constellations of the Serpent <note>Ashlesha</note>, Krittika, or Shatabhisha, on the days of the Sun, Mars, or Saturn, during the lunar days known as Bhadra, is indeed a maiden named Poison." And so in the *Muhurta Ganapati*: "On the days of the Sun, Mars, and Saturn, if the lunar day is Bhadra and the constellation is Shatabhisha, Ashlesha, or Krittika, the woman born there is a Poison Woman. (1)"

->And so in the *Muhurta Ganapati*:<-
->When a malefic planet is situated in the rising sign in an enemy's house...<-

Even if two benefic planets are present in this combination, she is born a Poison Maiden. ||37||

**Meaning—** A woman born in a configuration where two malefic planets are situated in the houses of their enemies within the birth ascendant <note>Lagna: the zodiac sign rising on the eastern horizon at the time of birth</note>, and where there is one malefic planet even if benefic planets are also in the ascendant, is considered a Poison Maiden <note>Visha Kanya: a girl born under specific inauspicious astrological alignments believed to bring misfortune to her husband</note>. ||37|| And so it is said in the *Light of the Three Worlds* <note>original: "Trailokya-prakasha"</note>: "When two planets are in enemy houses and a benefic is in the ascendant, but one malefic is also there, the woman born is a Poison Maiden." ||1|| Furthermore, in the *Ornaments of Nativity* <note>original: "Jataka-alankara"</note>: "If two benefic planets are in the ascendant along with one malefic, and two other planets have entered their enemy signs at birth, then that maiden is called 'Poisonous'." ||2||

->Furthermore, the Combination for a Poison Maiden from the *Ornaments of Nativity*<-

If birth occurs on a Saturday coinciding with the *Ashlesha* constellation and the second lunar day; or on a Tuesday with the *Shatabhisha* constellation and the seventh lunar day; or on a Sunday with the *Vishakha* constellation and the twelfth lunar day, she is called Poisonous. If at the time of birth, the son of the Earth <note>Mars</note> is in the ninth house, the son of Saturn <note>Saturn</note> is in the ascendant house, and the Sun is in the fifth house, that maiden is called Poisonous. ||38||

**Meaning—** First combination: Saturday, *Ashlesha* constellation, and the second lunar day. Second combination: Tuesday, *Shatabhisha* constellation, and the seventh lunar day. Third combination: the twelfth lunar day, *Vishakha* constellation, and Sunday. A girl born in any of these three combinations is called a Poison Maiden. ||1|| And one in whose birth chart Mars is in the ninth house, Saturn is in the ascendant, and the Sun is in the fifth house—a maiden born in such a combination is called a Poison Maiden. ||38|| And so in the *Birth of Combinations* <note>original: "Yoga-jataka"</note>: "Saturn in the ascendant, the Sun in the house of the son <note>the 5th house</note>, and the son of Earth <note>Mars</note> in the house of Dharma <note>the 9th house</note>; a woman born in this combination becomes a Poison Maiden." ||1|| As stated in the *Lord of Auspicious Times* <note>original: "Muhurta-ganapati"</note>: "She who has Saturn in the ascendant, the Sun in the fifth, and Mars in the ninth is also called Poisonous; such various types of Poison Maidens should not be wed." ||1||

<vocab>Poison Maiden <term>Visha Kanya/Vishangana: an inauspicious astrological category</term>, Astrology <term>Jyotish: the science of light/astrology</term>, Ascendant <term>Lagna: the rising sign</term>, Constellation <term>Nakshatra: lunar mansion</term>, Ornaments of Nativity <term>Jataka-alankara: a classical text</term>, Birth of Combinations <term>Yoga-jataka: an astrological treatise</term>, Lord of Auspicious Times <term>Muhurta-ganapati: a text on electional astrology</term></vocab>

It cites several classical authorities to warn against the marriage of women born under these specific malefic alignments.</summary>

### Now, the Exceptions to the Poison Maiden Affliction

> If at the time of birth, either from the Ascendant <note>Lagna: the zodiac sign rising on the eastern horizon</note> or from the Moon, a benefic planet or the Lord of the Seventh House is situated in the seventh house, it destroys the fault of childlessness, the fault of widowhood, and the so-called Poison Maiden affliction. ||39||

Meaning—In the birth chart of a girl, if at the time of birth a benefic planet or the Lord of the Seventh House <note>The house associated with marriage and partners</note> is seated in the seventh house from either the Ascendant or the Moon, then it destroys the fault of widowhood, the fault of being childless, and the Poison Maiden affliction. ||39||

### Also stated in the *Ornament of Horoscopy* <note>original: "Jatakalankara"</note>

> If a benefic or the Lord of the Seventh is in the seventh from the Ascendant or the Moon, it certainly destroys the Poison-named fault, childlessness, and subsequently the fault of widowhood. Thus should it be understood by wise astrologers through the entire collection of planetary combinations; this opinion is declared here in horoscopy by the noble ones, following the consensus of the sages, for the sake of those born.

Meaning—If a benefic planet is seated in the seventh from the Ascendant or the Moon, that is one combination. Or, if the Lord of the Seventh House from the Ascendant or the Moon is seated in the seventh house, then it continuously destroys the Poison Maiden affliction, the fault of childlessness, and the fault of widowhood. Having understood this through the knowledge of the experts of the science of light <note>Jyotish/Astrology</note>, and having taken the consensus of all the ancient sages, the wise should declare the results of the planetary combinations at the time of a person's birth. ||40||

### Remedy for the Poison Maiden Affliction: from *Ganesha’s Auspicious Times* <note>original: "Muhurta Ganapati"</note>

> Having performed the vow of Savitri <note>Savitri Vrata: a ritual fast and prayer dedicated to the goddess Savitri to ensure a husband's longevity</note> for the sake of averting widowhood, and having first married her to a Peepal tree or similar, one should then give her to a long-lived groom. ||41||

Meaning—If the fault of widowhood or the Poison Maiden affliction exists in a woman's birth chart, then that girl should perform the Savitri vow for the removal of the widowhood fault. Alternatively, before marrying that girl to a groom, she should be married to a Peepal tree <note>original: "Ashvattha"; Ficus religiosa</note> or...

<summary>This page details astrological conditions that cancel out the "Poison Maiden" (Vishakanya) affliction and provides ritual remedies, such as the Savitri vow and symbolic marriage to a tree, to ensure a long-married life.</summary>

After performing a symbolic marriage ceremony with a *Shalagrama* <note>A sacred stone representing the god Vishnu</note> or an image of Vishnu, the daughter should then be given in marriage to a long-lived groom. || 41 ||

### Now, the Combination for Infertility

> If the Sun and Moon are in the eighth house in their own signs... Infertility—

Meaning—If, at the time of a woman's birth, the Sun and Moon are situated in the eighth house <note>The house associated with longevity and hidden obstacles</note> in their own respective zodiac signs, that woman becomes infertile.

### Now, the Combination for "Crow-Infertility"

> —And if the Moon and Mercury are so placed, she becomes a "Crow-Barren" woman. || 42 ||

Meaning—If, at the time of a woman's birth, the Moon and Mercury are situated in the eighth house from the ascendant in their own signs, that woman becomes a <term>Crow-Barren woman</term> <note>A traditional term for a woman who conceives only once in her lifetime</note>. || 42 ||

### Now, the Combination for Infertility from the *Vira Jataka*

> If the Ascendant is in a house of Saturn or Mars, and the Moon is joined with Venus,
> and aspected by malefic planets, that woman attains infertility. || 43 ||

Meaning—If, at the time of a woman's birth, the Moon is situated with Venus in a sign belonging to Saturn or Mars (Capricorn, Aquarius, Aries, or Scorpio) and is viewed by malefic planets, then that woman becomes infertile. || 43 ||

### Now, the Combination for Stillbirth

> With the Sun, she is said to have dead offspring; likewise with Rahu.

Meaning—If, at the time of a girl's birth, the Sun or Rahu <note>The North Node of the Moon, considered a shadow planet</note> is seated in the seventh house and is viewed by Saturn, then that woman's children will die after being born.

### Now, the Combination for the Birth of Daughters

> If the Moon and Mercury are so placed, that woman shall give birth to daughters.

Meaning—If the Moon and Mercury are situated in the seventh house of a woman's chart and are aspected by Saturn, then that woman will give birth to female offspring.

### Now, the Combination for Miscarriage

> If Mars is situated in the seventh house and is also aspected by Saturn ||44||
> She should be known as one who suffers miscarriages; if Saturn is present, the offspring will be sickly ||45||

Meaning—If, at the time of a woman's birth, Mars is situated in the seventh house and is aspected by Saturn, then that woman suffers from miscarriages <note>original: "garbhasrava"</note>. Furthermore, if Mars is situated in the seventh house along with Saturn, then that woman gives birth to sickly children. ||45||

### Another Combination for Stillbirth

> And with Venus and Jupiter, children are born dead—

Meaning—If, at the time of a woman's birth, Venus and Jupiter are situated in the eighth house, then that woman has stillborn children.

### Another Combination for Miscarriage

> —With Mars, a miscarriage occurs ||46||

Meaning—And if Venus, Jupiter, and Mars are all situated in the eighth house, the combination for miscarriage <note>original: "garbhasrava yoga"</note> is formed. ||46||

### Now, the Combination for Widowhood

> With Mars in the twelfth or eighth house joined with malefic planets, and Rahu in the Ascendant;
> Or with the Sun and Mars in the Ascendant, she becomes a widow; with Saturn, she is ill-fated ||47||

Meaning—If, at the time of a girl's birth, Mars is in the twelfth or eighth house accompanied by malefic planets <note>planets like Saturn or Rahu that are believed to have a cruel influence</note>, and Rahu is seated in the Ascendant <note>the first house or "Lagna"</note> joined with a malefic, that woman becomes a widow <note>original: "randa"</note>. Also, if the Sun and Mars are in her birth Ascendant, she becomes a widow. If the aforementioned combinations occur while joined with Saturn, she becomes ill-fated and a widow. ||47||

## Another Combination: The Yoga of Widowhood

> If Rahu <note>the North Node of the Moon</note>, the Sun, and Mars are situated in the Ascendant <note>the first house or 'body'</note>, the woman becomes a widow. If, in addition to this combination, Venus is situated in the second house, she desires another husband. ||48||

**Meaning—** If at the time of a woman's birth, Rahu, the Sun, and Mars are located in the birth ascendant, that woman becomes a widow. If the aforementioned combination exists and Venus is also located in the second house, then after marriage, that woman desires another husband. ||48||

## Now, the Combination for Death Before the Husband

> Likewise, if cruel planets <note>malefics</note> are in the eighth house from the ascendant and auspicious planets <note>benefics</note> are in the second house, that woman dies before her husband. If the Moon is in Taurus, Leo, or Scorpio in the fifth house, she has few sons. ||49||

**Meaning—** If at the time of a woman's birth, sinful planets <note>malefics like Saturn or Mars</note> are seated in the eighth house from the birth ascendant, and auspicious planets are seated in the second house, that woman attains death before her husband. And she who has the Moon situated in the fifth house in the signs of Leo, Taurus, or Scorpio will have few sons. ||49||

## Now, the Combination for the Destroyer of Father’s and Father-in-law’s Families (from Shaunaka):

> A girl born in the Virgo ascendant with the Moon positioned between two sinful planets destroys her entire paternal family and her father-in-law’s family completely. ||50||

**Meaning—** If at the time of a woman's birth, the Moon is seated between sinful planets <note>a condition known as Papa-Kartari Yoga</note> in the Virgo ascendant, that woman brings an end to her father’s entire lineage and her father-in-law’s lineage without exception. ||50||

## Another from the Siddhanta Sara: The Combination for Many Sons

> At the time of birth for women, Mars, Saturn, and Rahu are auspicious in the centers <note>houses 1, 4, 7, 10</note> or trines <note>houses 1, 5, 9</note>. The Moon is auspicious in the seventh house, and Mercury, Venus, and Jupiter are auspicious in all houses.

**If the Lord of the Ascendant is in the house of desire, and the Lord of the seventh house is strong in the Ascendant, and the Lord of gains is in the house of children, the great sages say she will have many offspring. (51)**

Meaning—In a woman's birth chart, <term>Mars</term>, <term>Saturn</term>, and <term>Rahu</term> <note>the North Node of the Moon</note> grant auspicious results when placed in the 1st, 4th, 7th, 10th, 9th, or 5th houses. # Regarding the Yoga of a Husband's Devotion
**If Jupiter and Venus are in the fifth house, she will have many sons; she will be fortunate, honored by her husband, virtuous, and devoted to her vows. (52)**

Meaning—If <term>Jupiter</term> and <term>Venus</term> are located in the <term>Fifth House</term> at the time of a woman's birth, she will have many sons. Such a woman is "Subhaga" <note>exceptionally fortunate/beloved</note>, honored by her husband, endowed with good qualities, and remains faithful to her husband. (52)

# Regarding the Yoga of an Unsteady Husband
**If Mercury, Saturn, and the Moon are together and aspected by Venus, her husband will be unsteady, fickle-minded, extremely restless, prone to confusion, and lacking in discernment.**

Meaning—If <term>Mercury</term>, <term>Saturn</term>, and the <term>Moon</term> are situated in a single house at the time of a woman's birth and are "aspected" <note>glanced at/influenced</note> by <term>Venus</term>, that woman's husband will be unsteady, of a wandering nature, constantly fickle, filled with delusions, and lacking in cleverness or wisdom. (53)

# Regarding Death by Falling from a Mountain Peak
**If the Sun and Mars are in the tenth or fourth houses from the Moon, death occurs by falling from a snowy mountain peak.**

Meaning—If at the time of a girl's birth, the <term>Sun</term> and <term>Mars</term> are in the tenth or fourth houses from the Moon...

If [the planets] are situated in this position, that woman dies by falling from a mountain.

### Now, the Combination for Death by a Well

**Mars, the Moon, and the son of the Sun (Saturn) located in the second, seventh, and fourth houses respectively, lead to death by a well or step-well.**
Meaning—If at the time of a girl's birth, <term>Mars</term> <note>Sanskrit: Bhauma</note> is in the second house, the <term>Moon</term> <note>Sanskrit: Indu</note> is in the seventh, and <term>Saturn</term> <note>Sanskrit: Arkasuta, literally 'son of the Sun'</note> is in the fourth, then that woman dies by falling into a <term>well</term> or a <term>step-well</term> <note>A traditional Indian masonry well with stairs leading down to the water</note>.

### Now, the Combination for Death by Bondage

**The Sun and Moon, joined with or aspected by malefic planets and situated in Virgo, lead to death by bondage.**
Meaning—If at the time of a girl's birth, the <term>Sun</term> and the <term>Moon</term> are joined with or aspected by <term>malefic planets</term> <note>Cruel or unfavorable planets like Mars or Saturn</note> and are situated in the <term>Zodiac sign of Virgo</term>, then that woman dies in <term>bondage</term> <note>Implying imprisonment or being tied up</note>.

### Now, the Combination for Death by Water

**If those two (Sun and Moon) are in dual-natured signs and located in the Ascendant, death occurs by water. ||54||**
Meaning—If at the time of a girl's birth, the <term>Sun</term> and <term>Moon</term> are situated in the 3rd, 6th, 9th, or 12th signs <note>Gemini, Virgo, Sagittarius, and Pisces—the 'dual-natured' or mutable signs</note> and are also in the <term>Ascendant</term> <note>Lagna; the rising sign on the eastern horizon</note>, then that girl dies by drowning in water by her own accord. ||54||

### Now, the Combination for Death by Dropsy

**If the son of the Sun (Saturn) has reached Cancer and the Moon has reached Capricorn, then death among women is said to be caused by dropsy. ||55||**
Meaning—If at the time of a girl's birth, <term>Saturn</term> is in the <term>sign of Cancer</term> and the <term>Moon</term> is in the <term>sign of Capricorn</term>, then that woman dies from <term>dropsy</term> <note>Jalodara; a medical condition involving the accumulation of fluid in the body, often associated with liver or heart failure</note>. ||55||

## Now, the Combination for Death by Weapon or Fire

> The Moon <note>original: "Nishakara"</note> situated between malefic planets in a sign of Mars <note>Aries or Scorpio</note> causes death by weapon or fire. This must be carefully considered at the time of inquiry, during the selection of the groom, and at the time of giving the bride in marriage. ||56||

Thus ends the fifth chapter, titled "Description of Various Combinations," in the *Stri Jataka* composed by the Royal Astrologer, Pandit Shyam Lal, son of Shri Baldev Prasad, the jewel of the Gauda lineage residing in the illustrious Vanshavareli.

**Meaning—**If at the time of a girl's birth, the Moon is positioned between malefic planets <note>a condition known as Papa-Kartari Yoga</note> and is located in a sign belonging to Mars (1 or 8, i.e., Aries or Scorpio), then that woman dies by means of a weapon or fire. This entire combination must certainly be reflected upon during the time of a query <note>horary astrology</note>, at the time of engagement, during the selection of the groom, and at the time of the marriage ceremony <note>original: "kanyadan," the ritual of giving the daughter away</note>. ||56||

Thus ends the fifth chapter, titled "Description of Auspicious and Inauspicious Combinations," in the *Shyamsundari* Hindi commentary written by the Royal Astrologer Pandit Shyam Lal, son of Shri Baldev Prasad, the jewel of the Gauda lineage residing in the illustrious Vanshavareli.

> If even a single Jupiter, purified in the six divisional charts <term>Shadvarga: a method of calculating planetary strength across six different sub-charts</term>, is placed in a quadrant <term>Kendra: the 1st, 4th, 7th, or 10th houses of a horoscope</term> and is aspected by the Moon, the woman born under this influence becomes a queen. # The Fifth Royal Union (Rajayoga)

->The Fifth Royal Union: 5<-

---

If Mercury is in the Ascendant <note>the first house or 'Lagna'</note> while in its sign of exaltation <note>Virgo</note>, and the Priest of the Gods <note>Jupiter</note> is situated in the eleventh house; then that woman becomes the wife of a king, famous on this earth among the company of women. ||5||

**Meaning—** In the birth chart of a woman, if Mercury is located in the Ascendant in its exalted state, and Jupiter is located in the eleventh house—a girl born in such a yoga becomes the wife of a king and becomes famous on earth in the company of women. ||5||

<term>Rajayoga: A planetary combination indicating royalty, high status, or great fortune.</term> <term>Budha: The planet Mercury.</term> <term>Brihaspati: The planet Jupiter, the preceptor of the gods.</term> <term>Shitakara: A poetic name for the Moon.</term> <term>Bhrigu: A name for Venus, referring to the lineage of the sage Bhrigu.</term> <term>Lagna: The Ascendant or the zodiac sign rising on the eastern horizon at birth.</term> <term>Uchcha: Exaltation; the specific sign where a planet is at its maximum strength.</term> <term>Somasuta: Mercury, literally 'son of the Moon'.</term> <term>Kalatraga: Situated in the seventh house, which governs the spouse.</term>

<summary>This page describes two specific astrological configurations (Rajayogas) that predict a woman will achieve royal status and fame. The fourth yoga involves the Moon, Venus, Mercury, and Jupiter, while the fifth focuses on an exalted Mercury in the first house and Jupiter in the eleventh.</summary>

# Female Horoscopy

## Now, the Sixth Royal Combination
### Sixth Royal Combination Chart 6

If Mercury <note>original: "Somasuta," literally the son of the Moon</note> is situated in the third house, Jupiter <note>original: "Devamantri," the counselor of the gods</note> is in the fourth house and purified in the six-fold divisional charts <term>Shadvarga: a system of six types of divisional charts used to determine the strength of a planet</term>, and Venus <note>original: "Bhrigu"</note> is in the ascendant <term>Lagna: the zodiac sign rising on the eastern horizon at the time of birth</term>, it makes a woman equal to a king and the possessor of many herds of horses. ||6||

**Meaning**—In the birth chart of a woman, if Mercury is placed in the third house, and Jupiter—purified in the six-fold divisions—is placed in the fourth house, and Venus is in the birth ascendant, then it makes that woman like a king, possessing many groups of horses. That is to say, the army of that queen consists of many cavalrymen. ||6||

# Now, the Eleventh Royal Yoga
## Chart for the Eleventh Royal Yoga

<note>The original text contains a diagram here showing Mars in the 3rd house, Saturn in the 11th, and Jupiter in a fixed Ascendant.</note>

## Now, the Fourteenth Royal Union (Raja Yoga)

->Chart for the Fourteenth Royal Union<-

<note>Astrological chart (Kundali) for the 14th Raja Yoga. It shows a Taurus (2) ascendant with the Moon (Moon) in the first house, Venus (Venus) in the eleventh house (Sign 12), and Mercury (Mercury) in the seventh house (Sign 8).</note>

### Now, the Yoga for a Husband Honored by Kings
<note>A "Yoga" in astrology is a specific planetary combination that produces a particular result.</note>

If the seventh house falls in an even sign, joined with auspicious planets and aspected by auspicious planets, the husband shall be honored by kings. || 22 ||

**Meaning—** At the time of a maiden's birth, if there is an even-numbered zodiac sign in the seventh house <note>The seventh house in a chart traditionally represents the spouse.</note> and it is occupied and viewed by <term>benefic planets</term><note>Naturally auspicious planets like Jupiter, Venus, or a waxing Moon.</note>, then that woman's husband will be worthy of honor by royalty. || 22 ||

### Now, the Yoga for being Adorned by Maidservants

When the Moon, Venus, and Mercury are in the Ascendant, or if those three are Jupiter, Venus, and the Moon; the woman, possessed of many types of happiness and virtues, shall be adorned by maidservants. || 23 ||

**Meaning—** At the time of a maiden's birth, if the Moon, Venus, and Mercury are situated in the Ascendant, or if Jupiter, Venus, and the Moon—these three—are situated in the Ascendant, that woman will be endowed with many joys and virtues and will be served by many female attendants. || 23 ||

### Now, the Characteristics of Husbands for Women

If the Sun is in the <term>house of setting</term><note>The 7th house, representing the husband.</note>, the husband is fair-bodied, lustful, and lotus-eyed. If the Moon is there, he is endowed with beauty and virtue, though lean of body, a seeker of pleasure, and prone to illness. If Mars is there, he is humble, of cruel disposition, lazy, but clever in speech and of a reddish complexion.

It details how specific planets in the Ascendant or Seventh House influence the spouse's physical appearance and temperament.</summary>

One who delights in the expansion of knowledge, wealth, and virtues, when Mercury is in the house of passion <note>the seventh house</note>. ||24||
Long-lived, with wealth and majesty equal to a king, and lustful in youth when Jupiter is present;
Handsome, always clever in joyful play, a poet, and a lord of the earth.
When Saturn is in the house of passion, the husband has an aged body and a trembling frame, and is a sinner;
When Rahu or Ketu <note>the North and South Nodes of the Moon</note> are situated there, he is of foul mind, base, or like a low-born person.

**Meaning—** For those women at whose birth the Sun is situated in the seventh house, the husband will have fair-colored limbs, be lustful, and have eyes filled with anger. If the Moon is in the seventh house, he will be handsome, virtuous, possessed of a weak body, a seeker of pleasure, and troubled by disease. If Mars is situated in the seventh house, he will be humble yet of a wicked nature, clever in speech, lazy, and of a reddish complexion. If Mercury is in the seventh house, he will be one who loves knowledge, wealth, virtue, and worldly affairs. ||24|| And if Jupiter is in the seventh house, he will be long-lived and possessed of wealth and majesty equal to a king; he will be beautiful, always clever in joyful games, a knower of poetry, and a lord of the earth <note>a ruler or landowner</note>. If Saturn is in the seventh house, he will have an aged body, a shaky frame, and be a sinner. If Rahu or Ketu is in the seventh house, he will be of foul intellect, base, or equal to a base person. ||25||

->Now, the Diagram named 'Dimbha' for the birth of a girl<-
->As stated in the *Svarodaya* <note>A classical text on the science of breath and sound</note><-

Place three stars <note>Nakshatras/lunar mansions</note> on the head and seven stars on the face;
Place eight stars on the two breasts and three stars on the heart. ||26||
Place three on the navel and three on the private parts, sequentially starting from the Sun's star.
This 'Dimbha' diagram for the birth of a girl was spoken by Brahma <note>the Creator</note> himself. ||27||

<term>Dimbha-akhya-chakram</term>: A symbolic diagram (Chakra) shaped like an infant or doll used to predict a child's future based on the position of stars at birth.
<term>Svarodaya</term>: An ancient Vedic science relating to the flow of breath and its connection to astrology and divination.
<term>Kanya-janmani</term>: At the birth of a girl.
<term>Saptama-sthana</term>: The seventh house in a horoscope, which governs marriage and the spouse.
<term>Nripa-tulya-vitta-vibhavah</term>: Possessing wealth and power comparable to a king.
<term>Nitya-vinoda-keli-chaturah</term>: One who is perpetually clever in amusements and play.
<term>Vriddha-kalevarah</term>: Having the physical appearance or body of an old man.

Meaning—The **Dimbha Diagram** <note>Dimbha-akhya Chakra; a symbolic figure of a child used for astrological mapping</note> is described for the time of a woman's birth. There are 3 stars <note>Nakshatras or lunar mansions</note> on the head, 7 in the mouth, 4 on each of the two breasts, and 3 in the heart. ||26|| There are 3 in the navel and 3 stars on the pubic region; these should be placed in order starting from the Sun's star. Lord Brahma has described this Dimbha Diagram for the time of a girl's birth. ||27||

## Now, the Results of the Stars' Positions in the Diagram

> If in the head, she will be filled with distress; in the mouth, endowed with grain and wealth.
> In the heart, she will possess happiness; in the private parts, the woman will be unchaste. ||28||

Meaning—If a girl's birth star falls on the head, it causes distress; if it falls on the mouth, it brings wealth and abundance of grain. If it falls on the heart, it brings happiness; if it falls on the pubic region, it makes her unchaste. ||28||

> A birth star falling on the breasts increases the husband's happiness.
> At the time of birth, if it is in the navel, she is dissatisfied yet devoted to her master. ||29||

Meaning—If a girl's birth star falls on the breasts, it increases the husband's happiness; if it falls on the navel, she remains dissatisfied but is devoted to her husband. ||29||

## Now, the Woman-Diagram as told in the Jatkabharana <note>A classic 15th-century Sanskrit treatise on horoscopy</note>

In the Woman-Diagram <note>Nari-chakra</note>, three stars are to be placed on the head and seven stars in the mouth. Four stars each should be placed on the breasts, and three stars are to be situated in the heart region. ||30|| Three should be given to the navel and three to the private parts; these are counted from the Sun's star to the Moon's birth star. There is true distress if the star is in the head; if situated in the mouth, there is a constant attainment of sweet foods and happiness. ||31|| There is a great increase in the husband's love if it stays on the breasts; if situated in the chest region, there is extreme joy. In the navel, there is an endless increase in the husband's affection; if situated in the private parts, there is a great excess of lust. ||32||

Meaning—Now the <term>Woman’s Wheel</term> <note>A symbolic diagram used in Vedic astrology to predict a woman's fortune based on the position of the Moon relative to the Sun's constellation</note> is described. The results are calculated from the Sun’s constellation to the Moon’s constellation. One should visualize the form of a woman and place 3 constellations starting from the Sun’s constellation on the head. Place 7 on the face, 4 on each breast, and 3 on the heart. ||30|| Place 3 on the navel and 3 on the private parts. If the Moon’s constellation falls on the constellations assigned to the head, there is distress; if the Moon falls on the face, there is a daily gain of sweets. ||31|| If the Moon falls on the breast constellations, say that there will be great affection from the husband. If the Moon falls on the heart, joy is attained. If the constellation falls on the navel, there is great anxiety for the husband. If it falls on the constellations of the private parts, she should be said to be afflicted by desire. ||32||

> One should apply these results during a query, at the time of marriage,
> during the engagement ceremony, and also at the time of birth. ||33||

# Now, the Chapter on the Results of Birth According to Lunar Days is Examined

->As stated by the astrologer Shyama<-

## Results of Birth on the First Lunar Day (Pratipada)

**When a woman is born on the first lunar day <note>original: "agnitithau" - the day of the fire-god Agni</note>, she is endowed with good fortune and is beloved by her husband. She possesses a virtuous character, is blessed with many sons and grandsons, and shines with the knowledge of the scriptures.**

Meaning—The woman who is born on the first lunar day <term>Pratipada: the first day of the lunar fortnight</term> is a woman joined with good fortune, dear to her husband, and possesses a character rooted in great merit. Along with many sons and grandsons, she is distinguished by her knowledge of the Vedas and Vedanta <note>The Vedas and Vedanta are the primary sacred scriptures of Hindu philosophy</note>.॥1॥

## Results of Birth on the Second Lunar Day (Dvitiya)

**A woman born on the second lunar day is beautifully dressed, possesses great radiance, is filled with compassion, and is beloved by the king. She has beautiful eyes and hair, is deeply devoted to many religious duties, and is charming to the mind.**

Meaning—The woman who is born on the second lunar day <term>Dvitiya: the second day of the lunar fortnight</term> is a woman of extreme radiance, wearing excellent attire, filled with mercy, and dear to the ruler. She has beautiful eyes and hair, is dedicated to many religious duties, and is a woman who understands the heart.॥2॥

## Results of Birth on the Third Lunar Day (Tritiya)

**Born on the third day, she is gentle, very truthful, slow-moving, and deliberate in her long-term actions. She is a woman devoted to pilgrimages, of noble birth, endowed with virtues, and possesses sons and grandsons.**

Meaning—The woman who was born on the third lunar day <term>Tritiya: the third day of the lunar fortnight</term> is a woman of great fortune and a faithful wife. She is slow and steady, performing her work over a long period of time. She is attached to visiting holy pilgrimage sites, is endowed with good qualities, and possesses sons and grandsons.॥3॥

<summary>This page details the astrological predictions for women born on the first three days of the lunar month, emphasizing virtues like religious knowledge, beauty, and family prosperity.</summary>
<keywords>Tithi, Pratipada, Dvitiya, Tritiya, female horoscopy, Shyama Daivajña, Vedic knowledge, family lineage, lunar astrology</keywords>

### Now, the Results for Birth on the Fourth Lunar Day

The woman born on the fourth lunar day (*Chaturthi*) is always cruel, extremely sharp-tongued, and filled with lustful desires—inclined toward misconduct. Devoted to gambling and devoid of both religious merit and discernment, such is the woman born on the fourth day. ||4||

**Meaning:** A woman born on the fourth lunar day has a nature prone to harming others. She possesses a sharp temperament and is inclined toward adultery due to her lustful nature. She is ready to engage in gambling <note>original: "dyuta"</note> and lacks both religious duty (*Dharma*) and the power of discrimination (*Viveka*). ||4||

### Now, the Results for Birth on the Fifth Lunar Day

Endowed with desired qualities, beloved by her kinsmen, of good character, and skillful in her duties; she is joined with happiness, devoted to helping others, and possesses a sense of detachment—she in whose birth the fifth lunar day (*Panchami*) occurs. ||5||

**Meaning:** She who is born when the fifth lunar day prevails is endowed with the virtues of her kin and is dear to her brothers. She is of excellent character, clever in her work, and lives with happiness. She is dedicated to the service of others and possesses a spirit of detachment <note>original: "virakta"; implying a spiritual or dispassionate nature</note>. ||5||

### Now, the Results for Birth on the Sixth Lunar Day

The woman born on the sixth day is deeply truthful, a leader among women, and beloved by the people. However, she has an excess of phlegm, is prone to anger, and is harsh; she is a great spender and her conduct lacks moral policy. ||6||

**Meaning:** The woman born on the sixth lunar day (*Shashti*) is a devoted wife <note>original: "pativrata"</note> and a leader among women, cherished by people. She suffers from an excess of phlegm <note>original: "shleshma," referring to the Kapha humor in Ayurvedic medicine</note>, is wrathful, and harsh in speech. She spends excessively and her physical conduct or constitution lacks adherence to moral policy (*Niti*). ||6||

### The Results of Birth on the Seventh Lunar Day

> The woman born on the seventh day of the sun <note>The seventh lunar day is known as Saptami</note> possesses large eyes, is charming, and is endowed with prudence. She is devoted to the gods and her teachers, possesses a generous nature for giving charity, adheres to disciplined vows, and is free from all pride.

**Meaning:** A woman born on the seventh lunar day has large eyes, understands the minds of others, is humble, and is devoted to the deities and her preceptors. She is of a charitable disposition, follows religious rules, and her ego has been cast away.

### The Results of Birth on the Eighth Lunar Day

> Fond of meat, addicted to drink, misshapen, of a wicked nature, and devoid of sons and wealth; she lacks compassion and has a distorted appearance—such is the woman born on the lunar day belonging to the Lord of Gauri <note>Gauri's husband is the god Shiva, who presides over the eighth day, Ashtami</note>.

**Meaning:** The woman at whose birth-time it is the eighth lunar day of Lord Shiva, the husband of Parvati, is fond of meat and intent on drinking intoxicants. She is unattractive, possesses a wicked temperament, is lacking in children and riches, is merciless, and has a frightening form.

### The Results of Birth on the Ninth Lunar Day

> Lacking in family, a harsh woman, averse to all household tasks; even as a maiden she is wicked and engaged in vices—she in whose birth the ninth lunar day occurs.

**Meaning:** The woman at whose birth-time it is the ninth lunar day <note>Navami</note> is devoid of family support, is harsh, and turns her face away from all household duties. Even before marriage, she is of ill-repute and is occupied with many vices.

<note>The Sanskrit verses provide the formal astrological rules, while the Hindi "Artha" (meaning) sections provide the vernacular explanation for the practitioner.</note>

## Results for Birth on the Tenth Lunar Day

> The woman born on the tenth lunar day (Dashami) is devoted to righteousness, possesses a fine mansion, has a long neck, and is eloquent of speech. She finds joy in worshipping the gods and is blessed with virtuous sons. ||10||

**Meaning—** The woman in whose birth time the tenth lunar day <note>Dashami: the 10th day of the lunar fortnight</note> occurs is dedicated to religious duties, owns a beautiful house, has a long neck and a lovely voice, is clever, takes pleasure in the worship of deities, and possesses good sons. ||10||

## Results for Birth on the Eleventh Lunar Day

> She is inclined toward the worship of gods and priests, fasting, and charity; her mind is focused solely on merit and she is skilled in noble deeds. Knowing the arrival of various meanings and scriptures, such is she whose birth date is the eleventh. ||11||

**Meaning—** She whose character is defined by the worship of deities and Brahmins <note>priestly class</note>, and by fasting and charity; whose mind is excellent and focused on merit; who is capable of performing noble deeds; who understands many types of meanings and is a scholar of the scriptures—the woman whose birth date is the eleventh <note>Ekadashi: a day often reserved for fasting and spiritual reflection</note> is a knower of Vedanta <note>the philosophical end of the Vedas</note>. ||11||

## Results for Birth on the Twelfth Lunar Day

> She takes pleasure in reservoirs of water, is of good character, and remains in her own home, endowed with grace. With the disposition of Indra, she observes the faults of others; she who is born on the twelfth is a leader among women. ||12||

**Meaning—** One who takes pleasure in lakes or reservoirs, is of good character, and resides in her own home. She is graceful and, like the god Indra <note>original: "Surendra"; Indra is said to have a thousand eyes to see everything</note>, she observes the hidden faults of others as if with a thousand eyes. She who is born on the twelfth lunar day <note>Dwadashi</note> is preeminent among women. ||12||

## Results of Birth on the Thirteenth Lunar Day

Endowed with beauty, devoted to righteousness, deeply truthful, a knower of the good scriptures, and
skilled in happiness; possessed of forgiveness, she conquers all enemy factions—such is the
woman who is born on the thirteenth lunar day <note>Sanskrit: Trayodashi; the 13th day of the waxing or waning moon</note>. ||13||

**Meaning—** The woman born on the thirteenth lunar day is endowed with beauty, intent on her religious duties, a devoted wife <note>original: "pativrata" - one who is faithful to her husband</note>, knowledgeable in the fine scriptures, possessed of excellent speech, clever, forgiving, and one who has completely conquered the ranks of her enemies. ||13||

## Results of Birth on the Fourteenth Lunar Day

Engaged in the play of the God of Love, skilled in her duties, of contrary behavior, bereft of husband and son;
she may live by her beauty, be stained, of bad character—the woman born on the fourteenth is indeed
of a strange and fickle mind. ||14||

**Meaning—** Engaged in the arts of love, clever in her work, but of crooked or contrary behavior, lacking a husband and son, earning her livelihood through the profession of a courtesan <note>original: "veshyavritti" - living by the trade of beauty</note>, unclean, of wicked character; the woman born on the fourteenth lunar day <note>Sanskrit: Chaturdashi; the day preceding the full or new moon</note> possesses the aforementioned qualities and a strange, unpredictable mind. ||14||

## Results of Birth on the Full Moon Day

Of very beautiful form, possessing good virtues, modest, virtuous, having fine sons, happy,
and a knower of the arts; large-eyed and moon-faced is she who is born on the
lunar date of the Full Moon, a beautiful beloved. ||15||

**Meaning—** Possessing a beautiful and noble form, endowed with fine virtues, modest, a devoted wife, having beautiful sons, happy, knowledgeable in the various arts, with large eyes and a face like the moon—the woman born at the time of the Full Moon <note>Sanskrit: Purnima</note> is a beautiful and beloved lady. ||15||

### Now, the Results of Birth on the New Moon Day

> The woman born on the New Moon day <note>original: "Darsha Tithi"</note> is very virtuous; she possesses a beautiful face, is a devotee of priests and deities, is beloved by her husband, and is disciplined, well-behaved, and clever. She is highly proficient in the systematic management of household duties. ||16||

Meaning—She who has a beautiful face, devotion toward Brahmins and gods, is dear to her husband, possesses a saintly and excellent character, is clever, and is skilled in performing household tasks according to proper methods—the woman in whose birth time the New Moon <note>Amavasya</note> occurs is considered meritorious. ||16||

### Now, the Chapter on the Results of Births on Weekdays is Described

# Female Horoscopy

### Now, the results for those born on Monday

> She is very smooth-skinned, fortunate, possessed of a beautiful smile, and adorned with fine jewelry. She is constantly fond of playing in the water; such a girl is born on the day of the Moon <note>Monday</note>.

Meaning—A girl born on Monday possesses a beautiful, supple body and excellent fortune. She has a wonderful laugh, wears the finest ornaments, and is daily inclined toward water-based recreations.

### Now, the results for those born on Tuesday

> Constantly fierce in her brilliance, ungrateful, and filled with anger. She is devoted to wearing saffron-dyed garments; such a girl is born on the day of Mars <note>Tuesday</note>.

Meaning—A girl born on Tuesday is daily characterized by a fierce or intense aura. she is ungrateful <note>literally: one who does not acknowledge favors</note>, prone to anger, and is particularly fond of wearing clothes dyed in the color of safflower <note>a reddish-orange hue associated with the planet Mars</note>.

### Now, the results for those born on Wednesday

> Delighting in both auspicious and inauspicious speech, she is sweet-spoken. Constantly devoted to righteous deeds; such a girl is born on the day of Mercury <note>Wednesday</note>.

Meaning—A girl born on Wednesday remains content regardless of whether she hears good or bad words. She speaks sweetly and is daily engaged in religious duties and virtuous actions.

### Now, the results for those born on Thursday

> Free from sinful deeds and endowed with wealth and grain. A woman who honors the gods and the twice-born <note>term for Brahmins or the priestly class</note> is she who is born on the day of Jupiter <note>Thursday</note>.

Meaning—A woman born on Thursday is free from all sinful activities. She is possessed of great wealth and food supplies, and she is dedicated to the worship of the deities and the priestly class.

<summary>This page outlines the personality traits and physical characteristics of women based on their birth on Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday, or Thursday, ranging from the watery grace of Monday to the fiery temperament of Tuesday.</summary>

### Now, the results for one born on Friday are stated:

->Endowed with fine clothes and ornaments, accompanied by elephants and horses,<-
->A virtuous woman, blessed with sons, is the girl born on Friday. ||6||<-

**Meaning**—She who is born on Friday is well-provided with clothing and jewelry, possesses wealth in the form of elephants and horses <note>In a modern context, this signifies great luxury and means of transport</note>, is a faithful wife, and is blessed with sons. ||6||

### Now, the results for one born on Saturday are stated:

->Unclean, poorly dressed, talkative, and a creator of enmity,<-
->With few sons and devoid of compassion is the girl born on the day of Saturn. ||7||<-

**Meaning**—She who is born on the day of Saturn (Saturday) is untidy, wears poor clothing, speaks fearlessly or harshly, creates animosity with everyone, has few sons, and is lacking in mercy. ||7||

->Pandit Shyam Lal, son of Shri Baldev Prasad,<-
->the jewel of the Gauda lineage residing in Vans-Bareilly. ||8||<-

# Chapter on the Results of Birth under the Lunar Mansions
## The results for birth under the Ashwini mansion according to the Elder Yavana:

> The woman born under Ashwini is knowledgeable of the mind, possesses great wealth, and has a lovely appearance.
> She speaks sweet words, is patient with everyone, and is delightful; she is endowed with intelligence and is devoted to the gods and teachers. (1)

**Meaning**—A woman born in the <term>Ashwini</term><note>The first lunar mansion, associated with the twin physicians of the gods and beginnings.</note> constellation is a knower of the mind, very wealthy, possesses a pleasing appearance, speaks kind words, is tolerant of all, extremely beautiful, possessed of intellect, and is devoted to the deities, teachers, and priests. (1) Furthermore, in another text: **"The girl is strong, always full of self-respect, and skilled in practical affairs; she is indeed born under the star of Daksha."**

## Now, the results for birth under the Bharani mansion:

> A woman born under Bharani is surrounded by groups of women, yet she is cruel and fond of quarreling.
> She possesses a very wicked heart, is devoid of wealth, lacks influence, and is constantly dressed in dirty clothes. (2)

**Meaning**—A woman born in the <term>Bharani</term><note>The second lunar mansion, ruled by Yama, the god of death; often associated with transformation and discipline.</note> constellation is surrounded by many women but has a nature that harms others; she loves conflict, has a thoroughly wicked heart, is destitute of riches, has lost her dignity, and is perpetually unkempt. (2) Furthermore: **"The girl is extremely happy, has beautiful limbs, is cheerful, and is praised by her mother and father; such is one born under the deity Yama."**

## Now, the results for birth under the Krittika mansion:

> A woman born under Krittika becomes excessively angry,
> devoted to conflict, and detached. She is hateful, devoid of kinsmen,
> has an excess of phlegm, and always has a slender body. (3)

**Meaning**—A woman born in the <term>Krittika</term><note>The third lunar mansion, known as the Pleiades, ruled by Agni, the god of fire.</note> constellation is possessed of great anger, is fond of fighting, is reclusive, always harbors enmity, is abandoned by her relatives, has an excess of <term>phlegm</term><note>Original: "Shleshma." In Ayurvedic medicine, this refers to the Kapha humor which governs structure and fluid.</note>, and is always physically weak.

<summary>This page details the astrological personality profiles for women born under the first three lunar mansions: Ashwini, Bharani, and Krittika, contrasting virtuous traits like intelligence and wealth with negative traits like anger and poverty.</summary>
<keywords>Female Horoscopy, Nakshatras, Ashwini, Bharani, Krittika, Vedic Astrology, character traits, Elder Yavana</keywords>

the body remains forever. <note>This appears to be the conclusion of a previous verse regarding physical health.</note> ||3|| Furthermore, in another text: "A girl born under Krittika is radiant, endowed with fame, but attached to others; she is a heavy eater and possesses a fierce form."

### Now, the results of being born under Rohini

> A woman born under the Rohini star has a large body, is pure, and is not intoxicated by pride.
> She considers her husband supreme, is devoted to her father and mother, and is endowed with good sons, daughters, and wealth. ||4||

**Meaning—** A woman born in the Rohini <term>Nakshatra; a lunar mansion or star cluster in Indian astrology</term> has a large physique, is pure, and is devoid of arrogance. Her husband is her primary focus; she is a devotee of her parents and is blessed with excellent sons, daughters, and prosperity. ||4||

In another opinion: "A girl born in Rohini is long-lived, possesses children, increases the prestige of her lineage, is fortunate, and is self-respecting."

### Now, the results of being born under Mrigashira

> Under Mrigashira, a woman is respectable, beautiful, and speaks with a pleasant voice;
> she loves ornaments. She knows various subjects, is devoted to the scriptures, has good sons,
> takes shelter in righteousness, has a fair body, and is diligent. ||5||

**Meaning—** A woman born in the Mrigashira constellation is beautiful, dignified, cheerful, and speaks sweet words. She wears jewelry, understands many subjects, is knowledgeable in the <term>Shastras; traditional Indian scriptures or treatises</term>, is accompanied by excellent sons, is intent on following the path of righteousness, and possesses a fair-complexioned body. ||5||

Furthermore— "A girl born under the deity of the Moon <note>original: "Somadaivate"; referring to Mrigashira, which is ruled by the Moon god Soma</note> is praised by her mother and father and shares in wealth; however, she may also be stingy and attached to others."

### Now, the results of being born under Ardra

> Under Ardra, a woman is filled with anger, possesses a wicked nature, and is prone to phlegm and bile.
> She has the temperament of the King of Gods <note>original: "Surendrabhava"; referring to Lord Indra</note>, is skilled at finding the faults of others, spends excessively, and is a 'false scholar' <note>one who pretends to be more learned than they are</note>.

# Female Horoscopy

Page 104

Meaning—A woman born in the **Ardra** <note>The sixth lunar mansion, associated with the star Betelgeuse and the deity Rudra</note> constellation is full of artificial pride, possesses a wicked nature, and suffers from imbalances of phlegm and bile <note>Referring to the Kapha and Pitta humors in Ayurvedic medicine</note>. Like the god Indra with his thousand eyes, she is ever-watchful of the faults of others. She spends excessively and is a "pretend scholar" who puts on a show of wisdom. ||6||

Furthermore—"A girl born under this fierce deity <note>Rudra</note> is addicted to sinful deeds, is unattractive, fond of quarreling, and harbors deep-seated animosity."

### Now, the results for one born in Punarvasu:

> The woman born in Punarvasu is devoid of deceit, possesses great learning through hearing, and has a naturally virtuous disposition. She is devoted to righteousness, charming, highly honored, and always remains with her husband. ||7||

Meaning—A woman born in the <term>Punarvasu Nakshatra</term> <note>The seventh lunar mansion, associated with the star Pollux and the goddess Aditi</note> is free from pride and has an excellent memory for what she has heard. She is of a meritorious nature, follows the path of religion, understands the minds of others, is worthy of worship by people, and is always blessed with a husband and good fortune. ||7||

Furthermore—"A girl born under this deity is characterized by a forgiving nature, is beloved by her kinsmen, is free from enmity, and is focused on the goals of the afterlife."

### Now, the results for one born in Pushya:

> The woman born in Pushya is beautiful, famous for her deeds, fortunate, and has a lovely form. She is devoted to the worship of gods and Brahmins, lives in a fine mansion, enjoys great happiness, and is the darling of her relatives. ||8||

Meaning—A woman born in the <term>Pushya Nakshatra</term> <note>The eighth lunar mansion, considered the most auspicious, associated with the star Delta Cancri</note> is beautiful and famous for her actions. She possesses excellent fortune and a lovely body. She worships the gods and the priestly class, is humble, owns a grand house, experiences abundant happiness, and is beloved by her brothers and kin. ||8||

### Now, the results for one born in Ashlesha

> The woman born under the Serpent star <note>Ashlesha</note> is ill-favored in appearance, overwhelmed by addictions or vices, devoid of pleasant deeds, and speaks with extreme harshness. She is a woman whose actions are devoid of truth, full of hypocrisy, devoted to sinful acts, and ungrateful. ||9||

Meaning—The woman who was born in the <term>Ashlesha Nakshatra</term> <note>The 9th lunar mansion, associated with serpent deities and often considered sharp or dread in nature</note> is possessed of many vices, lacking in dear or meritous actions, a speaker of very harsh words, a performer of deeds lacking in truth, filled with anger, intent on sinful work, and one who does not acknowledge favors done for her. ||9||

As it is said—"A girl born under the Serpent deity <note>original: 'Naga-daivate'</note> is fierce, ungrateful, ill-favored, fond of quarrels, yet deeply attached to her affections."

### Now, the results for one born in Magha

> In Magha, a woman becomes respectable, a partisan of many sciences and scriptures, possessing great wealth, and free from sin. She is a devotee of her elders, humble toward the twice-born <note>the priestly or learned class</note>, and endowed with royal happiness. ||10||

Meaning—A woman born in the <term>Magha Nakshatra</term> <note>The 10th lunar mansion, governed by the Ancestors (Pitris) and associated with royal power and tradition</note> is respected by good men, knowledgeable in many scriptures, possessed of prosperity, free from sin, humble toward Brahmins, a devotee of her teachers and elders, and joined with the comforts provided by kings. ||10||

Furthermore—"That girl is attached to eating costly food, enjoys many pleasures, and is devoted to the worship of ancestors and gods when born under the Ancestral deity."

### Now, the results for one born in Purva Phalguni

> Conquering enemies through her good fortune, blessed with beauty and grace, having good sons, endowed with prudence, and expert in good conduct; she is attached to the scriptures and a speaker of pleasant words...

That woman is perpetually of beautiful radiance, and her fame and physical well-being grow through great happiness.

## Now, the results of being born under Chitra

> Under Chitra, she wears wonderful ornaments and is beautiful, provided she is not born on the fourteenth lunar day. If born on that day in the dark fortnight, she becomes a 'Poison-Maid'; if in the bright fortnight, she becomes poor and unchaste. ||14||

Meaning—A girl born in the Chitra <term>nakshatra (lunar mansion)</term> possesses wonderful ornaments and beauty. However, the fourteenth day of the lunar month <note>Chaturdashi</note> should not be her birthday. If she is born on the fourteenth day of the dark fortnight <note>the waning moon</note>, she becomes a <term>Vishakanya (Poison-Maid)</term> <note>A woman believed in ancient astrology to bring misfortune or death to a husband</note>. If she is born in the Chitra nakshatra on the fourteenth day of the bright fortnight <note>the waxing moon</note>, that woman becomes impoverished and sinful. ||14||

> Furthermore—"A girl born under the deity Tvashtar <note>The celestial architect and deity of Chitra</note> wears white garments, is fond of laughter and lovers, and is devoted to the worship of ancestors and gods."

## Now, the results of being born under Swati

> Born under Swati, a woman is always wealthy in sons, more distinguished than others, truthful, wealthy, moderate in drink, endowed with fame, possessing many friends, and one who has conquered her enemies. ||15||

Meaning—A woman born in the Swati nakshatra is one who is constantly blessed with sons, is clever, truthful, and wealthy. She drinks little, is possessed of glory, has many friends, and is one who has defeated the ranks of her enemies. ||15||

> Furthermore—"A girl born under the deity of the Wind <note>Vayu, the presiding deity of Swati</note> is free from laziness, extremely beautiful, though sometimes held in contempt, victorious, and vigilant."

# Now, the results for those born under the Vishakha star are stated:

*A woman born under the Vishakha star will be influenced by the power of her friends, possess a very delicate body, and be endowed with great wealth. She is devoted to visiting holy pilgrimage sites <note>original: "tirtha"</note>, skilled in the performance of religious vows and duties, and is beloved by her kinsmen.* ||16||

**Meaning—** A girl born in the Vishakha constellation <note>The 16th lunar mansion, associated with the deities Indra and Agni</note> is influenced by her friends, has a tender body, is possessed of riches, is attached to holy places, is clever in religious fasts and duties, and is dear to her relatives. ||16||

**Furthermore—** "A girl born under this dual-deity star <note>Vishakha is also known as 'Dvi-daivata' or 'Two-Godded'</note> is rooted in righteousness, modest, endowed with the wealth of wisdom, and a speaker of truth."

# Now, the results for those born under the Anuradha star are stated:

*Born under the star of Friendship <note>Anuradha is ruled by the deity Mitra, the god of friendship</note>, she has good friends, is free from pride, possesses a cheerful appearance, and is endowed with authority. She wears modest dress and ornaments, has a slender waist, and is devoted to her elders and her husband.* ||17||

**Meaning—** A girl born in the Anuradha constellation <note>The 17th lunar mansion</note> is accompanied by good friends, devoid of ego, always has a pleasant countenance, and possesses sovereignty. Her form is characterized by humility; she wears fine ornaments and is always a devotee of great men <note>teachers or elders</note> and her husband. ||17||

**Furthermore—** "A girl born under the star of the Friend <note>Anuradha</note> may also be a heavy eater, possessed of greed, fond of wine and meat, and attached to others."

# Now, the results for those born under the Jyeshtha star are stated:

*A woman born under Jyeshtha is beautiful, bold, speaks very sweet words, and is filled with humility. She is prone to great anger, yet is fortunate, wealthy in children, dear to her kinsmen, and endowed with truthfulness.* ||18||

**Meaning—** A girl born in the Jyeshtha constellation <note>The 18th lunar mansion, meaning 'The Eldest'</note> is beautiful and speaks fearless sentences. Her speech is excellent; she is humble yet prone to much anger, and is excellent—

She is knowledgeable in many scriptures, is abandoned by her relatives, relies on others for support, and is filled with a very low or mean sort of pride. || 19 ||

**she shall always be possessed of fame. || 20 ||**

Meaning—A girl born in the Purva Ashadha Nakshatra is favorable to her family, the foremost among her kinsmen, a performer of worshipful deeds, very powerful, a devoted wife <note>original: "pativrata"</note>, possesses large eyes, is endowed with extraordinary beauty, and is famous in the world. || 20 ||

Furthermore—"A girl born under the Water deity is of a religious nature, modest, a speaker of truth, and devoted to meritorious deeds."

### Now, the results for one born under the Uttara Bhadrapada star:

-> Born under the penultimate star, she is devoted to her husband's welfare, endowed with forgiveness, and brings joy <-
-> to her elders. Her pride is stilled; she possesses the happiness of children, is discerning, <-
-> and is always devoted to the truth. ||26|| <-

Meaning—A girl born in the Uttara Bhadrapada <note>The 26th lunar mansion, associated with stability and restraint</note> nakshatra is always ready to do what is beneficial for her husband. She is forgiving, affectionate toward her elders, and her ego is tranquil. She possesses the joy of having sons, is clever/wise, and is ever-devoted to the truth. ||26||

> Furthermore—“One born under the deity Ahirbudhnya <note>The serpent of the depths, the presiding deity of this star</note> is of excellent intellect, attached to righteousness (Dharma), and endowed with good qualities and character.”

### Now, the results for one born under the Revati star:

-> Under the star of Pushan, she is well-nourished, has a large circle of friends, is naturally pure, and observant of vows. <-
-> She is endowed with luster, rich in many four-footed animals, <-
-> a destroyer of enemy factions, and lovely to behold. || <-

Thus ends the ninth chapter, titled "Description of the Results of Birth Stars," in the *Stri Jataka* (Women's Horoscopy) composed by Pandit Shyam Lal, the Royal Astrologer, son of Shri Baldev Prasad, the crowning ornament of the Gauda lineage residing in Vans Bareilly. ||9||

Meaning—A woman born in the Revati <note>The 27th and final lunar mansion, associated with journeys and nourishment</note> nakshatra is physically well-developed and has many friends and female companions. She is pure by nature, performs penance and religious vows, and possesses great brilliance. She is endowed with many four-footed animals <note>Historically referring to cattle and horses; in a modern context, wealth and vehicles</note>, destroys the ranks of her enemies, and is beautiful to look upon. ||27||

> Furthermore—“A girl born under the deity Pushan <note>The protector of flocks and herds, deity of Revati</note> serves her mother, father, and mother-in-law, honors gods and priests, and is of a favorable/kindly disposition.”

-> Thus ends the ninth chapter, titled "Description of the Results of Stars," <-
-> in the *Shyamsundari* Hindi commentary written by Pandit Shyam Lal, <-
-> Royal Astrologer, son of Shri Baldev Prasad, <-
-> ornament of the Gauda lineage of Vans Bareilly. ||9|| <-

# Now, the Chapter on the Results of Birth under Specific Yogas is Defined

->As stated by the astrologer Shyam<-

->Now, the results of being born under the Vishkambha Yoga are described<-

In the Vishkambha <note>The first of the 27 astrological 'Yogas' or lunar periods; the name literally means 'a pillar' or 'support'</note> Yoga, a woman born is well-bred, endowed with the happiness of sons and family, and beloved by her husband. She is independent in her actions, skilled in household management, generous of heart, and always humble. ||1||

Meaning—The woman who is born in the Vishkambha Yoga is possessed of the happiness of sons, friends, and so forth; she is dear to her master <note>husband</note>, independent in all her affairs, clever in domestic work, noble-minded, and consistently characterized by humility. ||1||

->Now, the results of being born under the Priti Yoga are described<-

The woman born from the influence of Priti <note>The second Yoga; the name means 'affection' or 'pleasure'</note> Yoga is knowledgeable in the good scriptures, possessed of wealth and grain, endowed with beauty, charitable, skillful, of a cheerful disposition, and beloved by the people. ||2||

Meaning—The woman who is born in the Priti Yoga is a knower of the good Shastras <note>sacred scriptures or sciences</note>, possessed of wealth and food-grains, endowed with beauty, a giver of charity, clever, always of a joyful body <note>healthy and cheerful</note>, and is dear to all people. ||2||

->Now, the results of being born under the Ayushman Yoga are described<-

In Ayushman <note>The third Yoga; the name means 'long-lived'</note> Yoga, the woman born shall indeed be long-lived, a mine of abundant luster, fond of forests, mountain fortresses, and rivers, humble in dress, and of a very virtuous character. ||3||

Meaning—The woman who is born in the Ayushman Yoga is of great age <note>long-lived</note>, possessed of much radiance, attached to forests, mountains, forts, rivers, and the like, wearing a dress of humility, and is very religious and well-behaved. ||3||

<summary>This page details the personality traits and life outcomes for women born under the first three astrological Yogas—Vishkambha, Priti, and Ayushman—emphasizing virtues like domestic skill, piety, and longevity.</summary>

### Now, the results of being born under the Auspicious Union (Saubhagya Yoga)

->A girl born in the Auspicious Union is fortunate, virtuous,<-

->She possesses a gentle smile, speaks kindly, and is<-
->always proud of the strength of her beauty. ||4||<-

Meaning—The girl who is born in the *Saubhagya Yoga* <note>Saubhagya literally means "good fortune" or "marital bliss."</note> is possessed of excellent destiny and intellect. She is committed to truth and is wealthy. Her laughter is soft and pleasant; she is a speaker of sweet words and remains perpetually proud of the power of her physical charms. ||4||

### Now, the results of being born under the Splendid Union (Shobhana Yoga)

->A woman born within the Splendid Union is endowed with beauty,<-
->quick to give good answers, possessed of wisdom,<-
->free from deceit in her person, blessed with sons,<-
->and expert in proper conduct. ||5||<-

Meaning—The woman who is born in the *Shobhana Yoga* <note>Shobhana translates to "bright," "brilliant," or "virtuous."</note> is filled with grace and is quick-witted in her responses. She is wise, and her body (or character) is free from hypocrisy. She is blessed with children and is highly skilled in social dealings and etiquette. ||5||

### Now, the results of being born under the Great Knot Union (Atiganda Yoga)

->Born in the Great Knot, a woman may be perceptive yet<-
->large-faced, arrogant, and prone to anger. She is a lover<-
->of strife, filled with wrath, ill-favored in appearance,<-
->lacking in discrimination, and overcome by vices. ||6||<-

Meaning—The woman who is born in the *Atiganda Yoga* <note>Atiganda is traditionally considered a malefic or "harsh" astrological period.</note> is one who knows the minds of others but has a large face. She is full of pride and anger. She loves to quarrel, is frequently furious, and possesses an unattractive form. She lacks the power of judgment and is devoted to various addictions or bad habits. ||6||

### Now, the results of being born under the Virtuous Action Union (Sukarma Yoga)

->A woman born in the Virtuous Action Union is exceedingly wise<-
->and proficient in all the arts.<-

### Now, the results for one born in Shiva Yoga

## Now, the Chapter on the Results of Birth in the Karanas is Described
### As stated by the Astrologer Shyam

->Now, the results of birth in the Vava Karana are told<-

> The woman born under the name of **Vava** is possessed of beautiful luster and form, fortunate, and of excellent character. Her home is endowed with all kinds of prosperity; she attains merit through her own actions and is truly grateful. ||1||

Meaning—A woman born in the <term>Vava Karana</term> <note>The Karana is an astrological period equal to half a lunar day (Tithi)</note> is possessed of a beautiful complexion and form, has excellent fortune, and is of a very noble disposition. Meaning—The woman born in the <term>Balava Karana</term> is endowed with intelligence and beautiful grace. > When a woman is born in the **Kaulava** Karana, she is certainly independent, having many friends and sons. She is compassionate, devoted to truth, confident, with very delicate limbs, and a speaker of pleasant words. ||3||

Meaning—The woman who is born in the <term>Kaulava Karana</term> is certainly independent—

> is devoted to helping others, and has a beautiful body. ||5||

**Meaning**—The woman who is born in the <term>Gara Karana</term> <note>The Gara Karana is the fifth moving Karana, associated with hard work and overcoming obstacles.</note> is very patient and consistently generous. She is well-versed in the sacred scriptures, has defeated the ranks of her enemies, is dedicated to the service of others, and possesses a fine physical form. ||5||

->Now, the results of being born in the Vanija Karana are told<-

> She whose birth is in Vanija is proficient and skilled
> in the work of commerce, and is rich in the arts. She is endowed with wisdom, rich in honor and ornaments,
> possesses a gentle smile, and is endowed with wealth and grain. ||6||

**Meaning**—The woman at whose time of birth the <term>Vanija Karana</term> <note>The Vanija Karana is the sixth moving Karana, traditionally associated with trade, business, and prosperity.</note> occurs is proficient and skilled in commercial activities. She is clever and filled with various arts, possesses wisdom, is adorned with honor and jewelry, has a soft smile, and is blessed with abundant wealth and food supplies. ||6||

->Female Horoscopy<-

### Now, the results for one born in the Vishti Karana:

> A woman born under the Bhadra <note>Bhadra is another name for the Vishti Karana, often considered inauspicious in Vedic astrology</note> is ill-favored in appearance, harsh in her speech, and mimics the mannerisms of men. She is devoid of affection, constantly dressed in dirty clothes, wicked, keeps bad company, and is prone to infidelity. ||7||

**Meaning:** The woman who is born in the Vishti Karana is ugly, speaks harsh words, has a masculine physique, is lacking in love, is perpetually unkempt, is of bad character, possesses wicked friends, and is an adulteress. ||7||

---

### Now, the results for one born in the Shakuni Karana:

> If born under the Shakuni, she is well-versed in the knowledge of omens <note>original: "shakuna-jnana"</note>, possesses an exceedingly graceful body, and is proficient in the science of mantras. She is prominent among many women, endowed with beautiful fortune, possessed of a multitude of virtues, and is always vigilant. ||8||

**Meaning:** The woman who is born in the Shakuni Karana is clever in the interpretation of omens, has an extremely beautiful body, is skilled in mantra-vidya <note>the science of sacred chants and rituals</note>, maintains friendships with many women, possesses excellent fortune along with a collection of good qualities, and is always alert. ||8||

---

### Now, the results for one born in the Chatushpada Karana:

> In the Chatushpada <note>literally "four-footed"</note>, a woman is humble, endowed with the strength of quadrupeds, and of good character. She is not given to hoarding, has a frail physical build, is lacking in proper conduct, and has a distorted appearance. ||9||

**Meaning:** The woman who is born in the Chatushpada Karana is humble, possessed of the strength of four-footed animals, and is of very good character. She does not accumulate wealth, has a weak body, is lacking in traditional righteous conduct, and possesses an unattractive form. ||9||

### Now, the results of being born in the Naga Karana are described:

> The woman born under the **Naga** <note>The 'Serpent' Karana</note> is intoxicated with pride, full of hypocrisy, and speaks harsh words. She possesses a wicked character, loves quarreling, is intent on malice, and is cruel. She is devoted to falsehood and brings ruin to her lineage. ||10||

**Meaning—** The woman who is born in the Naga Karana is arrogant, deceitful, and speaks wicked language. She has a bad disposition and loves to fight; she is always ready for enmity, possesses a hard heart, is attached to lying, and acts as a destroyer of her own family. ||10||

### Now, the results of being born in the Kinstughna Karana are described:

> The woman born in **Kinstughna** is bold in speech; her mind views righteousness and unrighteousness as equal. She shows no stability in either friendship or enmity, and she is always weak in her limbs and in the arts of love <note>original: "ananga" referring to Kamadeva/desire</note>. ||11||

**Meaning—** The woman who is born in the Kinstughna Karana speaks with great boldness. Her intellect treats virtue and vice as the same. She remains the same in friendship and in hostility <note>meaning she is inconsistent or indifferent to both</note>, and she is always physically weak and lacking in the passion of desire. ||11||

->Thus ends the eleventh chapter, titled "The Description of Results of Birth in the Karanas," in the *Stri Jataka* <note>Horoscopy of Women</note> composed by the Royal Astrologer, Pandit Shyam Lal, son of Shri Baldev Prasad, the jewel of the Gaur lineage residing in Vans-Bareilly.<-

->Thus ends the eleventh chapter, titled "The Description of Results of Birth in the Karanas," in the *Shyam Sundari* Hindi Commentary written by the Royal Astrologer, Pandit Shyam Lal, son of Shri Baldev Prasad, the jewel of the Gaur lineage residing in Vans-Bareilly.<-

# Now, the Chapter on the Results of the Rising Sign is Examined
## The Elder Yavana <note>Vriddha Yavana refers to an ancient authority in Indian astrology, often associated with the transmission of Greco-Bactrian astrological techniques into Sanskrit.</note>

### Now, the results for a birth in the Aries rising sign are stated:

> If the rising sign is Aries, the woman will be devoted to truth but cruel, and always prone to anger. She will have an excess of phlegm, speak with harsh words, and remain forever detached from her own kinsmen. ||1||

Meaning—The woman who is born in the Aries rising sign <term>Mesha-lagna: Aries as the ascending zodiac sign</term> is dedicated to truth, fearless, always possessed of anger, of a phlegmatic temperament, a speaker of harsh words, and remains perpetually alienated from her circle of relatives. ||1||

### Now, the results for a birth in the Taurus rising sign:

> If the rising sign is Taurus, the woman is devoted to truth, pleasing to the mind, humble in her actions, and beloved by her husband. She becomes skilled in all arts, devoted to her relatives, and a devotee of Brahmins and the gods. ||2||

Meaning—The woman who is born in the Taurus rising sign <term>Vrisha-lagna: Taurus as the ascending zodiac sign</term> is dedicated to truth, intuitive of the mind's workings, possesses a humble nature, is dear to her husband, clever in all the arts, devoted to her kinsmen, and a worshiper of the priestly class and the divine. ||2||

### Now, the results for a birth in the Gemini rising sign are stated:

> In the third rising sign, the woman speaks very harsh words, is devoid of desire, and lacking in virtues. She is always cruel, afflicted by phlegm and wind, prone to great expenses, and behaves with fierce actions. ||3||

Meaning—The woman who is born in the Gemini rising sign <term>Mithuna-lagna: Gemini as the ascending zodiac sign</term> is a speaker of harsh words, lacking in sexual desire, devoid of good qualities, always fearless <note>The Sanskrit 'nrishamsa' can imply cruelty or being 'man-slaying,' here interpreted as a fierce or ruthless independence</note>, possessed of a phlegm-and-wind temperament <term>Kapha-Vata: two of the three bodily humors in Ayurvedic medicine</term>, spends a great deal of money, and possesses a terrifying or formidable demeanor. ||3||

<summary>This page details the astrological personality profiles for women born under the first three zodiac signs—Aries, Taurus, and Gemini—according to the Elder Yavana tradition. It categorizes their character, physical temperament (doshas), and social behavior based on their rising sign.</summary>
