### The Results of Birth in Cancer Rising

If a woman is born when the sign of Cancer <note>original: "Karkalagna" or "Kulira"</note> is rising, she will be endowed with great humility. She is beloved by her kinsmen, possesses a virtuous and well-behaved character, is clever, blessed with children, and surrounded by all forms of happiness. ||4||

**Meaning—** The woman who is born in the Cancer ascendant is very polite, dear to her brothers, possessed of good conduct, clever, and along with her offspring, she remains endowed with all comforts. ||4||

### The Results of Birth in Leo Rising

With Leo <note>original: "Simhalagna"</note> as the ascendant, a woman becomes very sharp-tempered, may have an abundance of phlegm <note>term: Phlegm (Kapha) — one of the three bodily humors in Ayurvedic medicine, associated with water and earth</note>, or be fond of quarreling. Her body may be afflicted by various ailments, yet she is always devoted to helping others. ||5||

**Meaning—** The girl who is born in the Leo ascendant is a woman of extremely sharp nature, possessing a phlegmatic constitution, one who loves to fight, whose body is accompanied by many types of diseases, and who is always ready to do favors for others. ||5||

### The Results of Birth in Virgo Rising

A woman born at the rising of Virgo <note>original: "Kanyalagna"</note> is endowed with good fortune and happiness, and is a well-wisher to others. Within her own community, she is deeply devoted to righteousness <note>term: Dharma — religious and moral duty</note>, has mastery over her senses, and is skilled in all the arts. ||6||

**Meaning—** The woman who is born in the Virgo ascendant is possessed of good fortune and joy; she is a benefactor, very devoted to religion within her circle, a conqueror of her senses, and clever in every art form. ||6||

### The Results of Birth in Libra Rising

In the ascendant known as Libra <note>original: "Tulalagna"</note>, a woman's actions take a long time to complete; she is very slow and lacking in affection. She is quite proud and devoid of luster...

### Now, the Results of Birth in Scorpio Ascendant

A woman born in the Scorpio Ascendant is beautiful of limb and pleasing to the eyes. She is of most virtuous character, a faithful wife, possessing many excellences, and always devoted to the truth.

**Meaning—** The girl who is born in the Scorpio Ascendant is a woman with a superior body in terms of beauty. Her eyes are givers of joy. Her character is rooted in the highest merit; she is a devoted wife <note>original: "pativrata"; a woman devoted to her husband</note>, abundant in good qualities, and always ready to uphold the truth. ||8||

### Now, the Results of Birth in Sagittarius Ascendant

The woman born at the rising of the Bow <note>Sagittarius</note> is brave in intellect and mimics the form of a man. She is achievable only through conciliation, yet by nature she is harsh, devoid of affection, and lacking in tenderness. ||9||

**Meaning—** The girl born in the Sagittarius Ascendant is a woman who is a hero among the wise—meaning she is extremely intelligent—but she possesses a masculine physical form. She can only be won over through peaceful means <note>original: "sama"; the diplomatic art of conciliation</note>, is harsh in her methods, lacks natural affection, and is devoid of humility or love. ||9||

**Meaning—** The woman born in the Capricorn Ascendant is one of excellent fortune and is devoted to the truth. She is attached to visiting holy pilgrimage sites and has destroyed the ranks of her enemies. She is the leader in all tasks...

## Now, the results for one born in the Ascendant of Aquarius

> If a woman is born when Aquarius is the Ascendant, she is clever from birth but afflicted by blood disorders or wounds. She constantly acts in opposition to her elders, spends excessively, yet is devoted to meritorious deeds, though she can be ungrateful. ||11||

**Meaning—**The woman who is born in the Aquarius Ascendant <term>Kumbha-lagna: the rising sign of Aquarius</term> is clever from the moment of her birth. She suffers from diseases or wounds caused by blood <note>original: "kshataja," referring to blood-related ailments or injuries</note>. Her actions are always contrary to the wishes of great men or elders. She is prone to high expenses, performs acts of religious merit, but is ungrateful toward favors received. ||11||

## Now, the results for one born in the Ascendant of Pisces

> A woman born under the rising of Pisces has many sons and grandsons; she is beloved by her husband and respected by her kinsmen and the world. She has beautiful eyes and hair, is devoted to the gods and priests, is endowed with policy, and is devoted to the service of her elders. ||12||

**Meaning—**The woman who is born in the Pisces Ascendant <term>Meena-lagna: the rising sign of Pisces</term> is blessed with many sons, grandsons, and descendants. She is dear to her husband and held in high regard by her brothers and society. Her eyes and hair are beautiful. She is a devotee of the gods and Brahmins <note>priestly class</note>, possesses humility and policy, and is dedicated to the love and service of her teachers and elders. ||12||

->Thus ends the twelfth chapter, titled "The Description of Results for Birth in the Ascendants," in the Shyamsundari Hindi commentary created by Pandit Shyam Lal, the Royal Astrologer and son of Shri Baldev Prasad, the jewel of the Gauda lineage residing in the excellent city of Bareilly. ||12||<-

# Now begins the chapter describing the results of the Moon's zodiac sign at a woman's birth—according to the Elder Yavana <note>The "Elder Yavana" refers to an ancient authority in Indian astrology, often associated with Indo-Greek astrological traditions.</note>

## Results of birth in the sign of Aries

When the Moon is situated in Aries, the woman born will be resolute, dedicated to her duties, and a leader. She is blessed with sons, devoted to affection, truthful, and always remains humble and loving toward her elders. ||1||

Meaning—The woman born in the sign of Aries is bold and mature, devoted to truth, a leader, accompanied by sons, eager in affection, a faithful wife, and always behaves with humility toward her elders. ||1||

## Results of birth in the sign of Taurus

When the Moon rests in Taurus, she is of excellent character, devoted to the study of knowledge, discernment, and the sacred scriptures. She is attached to visiting holy pilgrimage sites, has many sons and grandsons, is beloved by her husband, and is skilled in the arts of love <term>Kamakala: the traditional arts and techniques of physical and emotional intimacy</term>. ||2||

Meaning—The woman at whose birth the Moon is situated in the sign of Taurus is clever in learning, devoted to the Vedas and Vedanta <note>The Vedas and Vedanta represent the highest philosophical and scriptural knowledge in the Hindu tradition</note>, fond of pilgrimages, accompanied by many sons and grandsons, dear to her husband, and skilled in the arts of love. ||2||

## Results of birth in the sign of Gemini

When the Moon is situated in Gemini, she is modest, possesses a beautiful body, and is lovely to behold. She is endowed with various types of wealth and honors, is wise, dedicated to helping others, and has eyes as beautiful as lotus petals. ||3||

Meaning—The woman at whose birth the Moon is situated in the sign of Gemini is a woman with a fine body and a lovely appearance; she possesses many kinds of wealth—

### Now, the results for those born under the sign of Libra

> When the Moon resides in the sign of the Balance <note>Libra</note>, a woman is born who is devoted to her vows, beneficial to her circle of kinsmen, faithful to her husband, blessed with sons, pleasing in character, and free from hypocrisy and excessive passion. ||7||

**Meaning**—The woman at whose time of birth the Moon is situated in Libra is a benefactor to her relatives, a devoted wife, accompanied by sons, a knower of the mind <note>intuitive or empathetic</note>, and is devoid of deceit and lustful artifice. ||7||

### Now, the results for those born under Scorpio are told

> When the Moon is in the sign of the Scorpion <note>Scorpio</note>, she keeps her thoughts well-guarded, possesses a steady temperament, and her actions are very clever. She is beneficial to her elders, observant of religious rules, possesses a vast treasury, and is free from pride. ||8||

**Meaning**—The girl at whose time of birth the Moon is situated in the sign of Scorpio is of a stable nature, possesses excellent and clever movements/actions, is helpful toward elders, follows disciplined vows, possesses much wealth, and her arrogance has been cast away. ||8||

> When the Moon is in the sign of the Archer <note>Sagittarius</note>, a woman becomes rich in her vows, intent on charity, and possesses beautiful red lips. She is a lover of songs, acts according to the welfare of living beings, has a lovely face, is a mother of daughters, and has destroyed her enemies. ||9||

**Meaning**—The girl at whose time of birth the Moon is situated in the sign of Sagittarius is endowed with religious observances, is prompt in giving charity, has red lips, loves music and singing, acts in accordance with the welfare of all creatures, has a beloved face, has destroyed her enemies, and is a mother of female offspring. <note>The text suggests she may specifically bear daughters or be a "mother of women."</note>

## Now, the results for those born in the sign of Capricorn

When the Moon is situated in Capricorn <note>original: "mrigasthe" - literally 'in the deer/goat-headed sign'</note>, the woman will have formidable teeth <note>original: "vikarala-danshtra" - often interpreted as large or prominent teeth</note>, be courageous, and possess a pleasing mind. ## Now begins the chapter on the results of the Sun and other planets in the twelve houses

### The results of the Sun situated in the First House <note>The First House, or "Tanu Bhava," represents the body and physical appearance.</note>

> If the Sun is in the first house at birth, it produces a woman with a harsh face, afflicted by severe ailments, of a wicked nature, very thin, ungrateful, fond of others' food, and lacking in luster. ||1||

**Meaning—** The woman at whose birth the Sun is situated in the Ascendant <note>The "Lagna" or rising sign</note> will have a sharp or fierce countenance, suffer from intense diseases, possess a malicious temperament, and have a weak or emaciated body. She will be unthankful for favors received, inclined to eat the food of others, and will lack physical radiance or vitality. ||1||

### The results of the Sun situated in the Second House <note>The Second House, or "Dhana Bhava," represents wealth, speech, and family.</note>

> The Sun situated in the house of wealth produces a woman devoid of money and grain, harsh in speech, lacking in devotion, fond of quarreling, engaged in hatred, wicked, and stripped of friendships. ||2||

**Meaning—** The woman at whose birth the Sun is situated in the house of wealth will be...

### Now, the results of the Sun situated in the Sixth House

->The Sun in the sixth house makes a woman bold, one who has destroyed<-
->the side of her enemies, clever, of peaceful conduct,<-

She is clever among women, possesses a peaceful nature, and finds joy in performing religious duties. She is dedicated to righteousness <term>Dharma: the path of cosmic order and duty</term>, possesses excellent fortune, and is beautiful. ||6||

### Now, the results of the Sun situated in the Seventh House

->When the Sun is in the house of setting, a woman is deprived of<-
->the state of marriage and all happiness. She is always<-
->fierce, devoid of affection, of a phlegmatic<-
->constitution, sinful, and ill-favored in appearance. ||7||<-

**Meaning**—The woman at whose time of birth the Sun is situated in the seventh house <note>The house of marriage and partnerships</note> is deprived of the marital state and is always devoid of all happiness. Her nature is perpetually filled with anger, she lacks humility or affection, possesses a phlegmatic <term>Kapha: one of the three bodily humors in Ayurvedic medicine</term> constitution, is prone to sin, and has an unattractive appearance.

**Meaning**—The woman at whose time of birth the Sun is situated in the eighth house <note>The house of longevity and hidden things</note> is endowed with poverty and sorrow along with her family members. She is engaged in sinful acts <insert>in all her deeds</insert>...

### Now, the results of the Sun situated in the Ninth House

The Lord of the Day <note>original: "vasarapa" - the Sun</note> situated in the house of Dharma <note>the 9th house, governing religion and fortune</note> produces a woman of poor conduct who is fond of reckless daring. She is devoid of good fortune, surrounded by many enemies, afflicted by diseases from birth, and lacking in wealth. ||9||

**Meaning—** The woman at whose time of birth the Sun is located in the ninth house is somewhat fond of daring <note>or impulsive actions</note>, is lacking in luck, has many enemies, is joined with many diseases, and is destitute of splendor or wealth. ||9||

### Now, the results of the Sun situated in the Tenth House

The Lord of the Day residing in the house of Action <note>the 10th house, governing career and public life</note> always produces a woman devoted to wicked deeds. She is devoid of influence, negligent in her duties, and exceedingly harsh or wicked by nature. ||10||

**Meaning—** If the Sun is situated in the tenth house for a woman, she is always intent on bad actions, lacks radiance or charisma, is lazy in her own work, and is naturally very wicked. ||10||

### Now, the results of the Sun situated in the Eleventh House

The Lord of the Day residing in the house of Gain <note>the 11th house, governing income and desires</note> makes a woman prosperous with many sons and grandsons. She is a master of her senses, expert in all arts, endowed with forgiveness, and honored by her kinsmen. ||11||

**Meaning—** The woman at whose time of birth the Sun is situated in the house of gain is wealthy and possesses many sons and grandsons. She is one who has conquered her senses <note>self-controlled</note>, is clever in all arts, possesses a forgiving nature, and is worshipped or respected by her relatives. ||11||

## Now, the results of the Sun situated in the Twelfth House

> If the Lord of the Day <note>the Sun</note> is in the twelfth house at a woman's birth, she is prone to wasteful spending, lacks humility, spends excessively, is devoted to drinking, is cruel, and her body is devoid of purity in all matters of consumption.

**Meaning**—The woman at whose time of birth the Sun is situated in the twelfth house <term>Dwadashabhava</term> <note>the house associated with loss, expenditure, and the subconscious</note> is one who spends wealth on wicked deeds, lacks modesty, is a spendthrift, is intent on drinking intoxicants, is fearless in a negative sense, eats what should not be eaten, and possesses a body lacking in cleanliness. ||12||

## Now, the results of the Moon situated in the Ascendant

> If the Moon is in the Ascendant <term>Lagna</term> during the bright fortnight, the woman born is of beautiful form; if in the dark fortnight, she is thin, very miserable, sickly, quarrelsome, and constantly dressed in dirty clothes.

**Meaning**—The woman at whose time of birth the Moon of the bright fortnight <term>Shuklapaksha</term> <note>the waxing phase of the moon, considered auspicious</note> is situated in the Ascendant is a woman of an extremely beautiful body. And if the Moon of the dark fortnight <term>Krishnapaksha</term> <note>the waning phase of the moon</note> is situated in the Ascendant, then that woman is miserable, afflicted with disease, has a quarrelsome nature, and is perpetually unkempt.

## Now, the results of the Moon situated in the Second House

> The Moon resting in the house of wealth produces a woman of abundant riches, for whom love is paramount, who is aligned with righteousness, skilled in her husband's service, highly modest, and a devotee of priests and gods. ||14||

**Meaning**—The woman at whose time of birth the Moon is seated in the house of wealth <term>Dwitiyabhava</term> <note>the second house, governing finances and speech</note> is a woman of great wealth. Humility is her primary trait; she acts according to righteousness <note>Dharma</note>, is clever in serving her husband, possesses great modesty, and is a devotee of the gods and the Brahmins <note>the priestly class</note>. ||14||

# With Hindi Commentary

## Now, the Results of the Moon in the Third House

The Moon in the third house produces a woman characterized by an excess of phlegm and wind <note>original: "kapha-vata-saram"; referring to the Ayurvedic humors</note>, who speaks harsh words. She is prone to anger, devoid of moral conduct, naturally wicked, miserly, and ungrateful. ||15||

**Meaning—** The woman in whose birth chart the Moon is seated in the third house suffers from diseases of phlegm and wind <note>The text mentions "atisara" or diarrhea/dysentery related to these humors</note>. She speaks harshly, remains in a state of anger, is lacking in ethics, is naturally ill-natured, stingy, and ungrateful. ||15||

---

## Now, the Results of the Moon in the Fourth House

The Moon situated in the house of happiness <term>Sukha-stha: the fourth house, representing the home and comforts</term> produces a woman endowed with great happiness and wonderful ornaments. She is of steady temperament, performs religious deeds according to the scriptures, enjoys many pleasures, and is devoted to the gods and her teachers. ||16||

**Meaning—** The woman in whose birth chart the Moon is located in the house of happiness <note>the fourth house</note> is possessed of much joy, wears extraordinary jewelry, has a stable nature, performs the duties prescribed by the Vedas, enjoys abundant comforts, and is devoted to the deities and the Brahmins <note>priestly or learned class</note>. ||16||

---

## Now, the Results of the Moon in the Fifth House

The "Cold-Rayed One" <note>original: "Shitakarah"; a poetic name for the Moon</note> residing in the house of progeny <note>the fifth house</note> makes a woman blessed with good sons and enriched with virtues and dignity. She possesses many servants, enjoys the happiness of children, is endowed with wealth, and is characterized by virtuous behavior. ||17||

**Meaning—** The woman in whose birth chart the Moon is located in the fifth house is blessed with excellent sons and possesses dignity and virtue. She has many servants, enjoys the happiness of having children, is wealthy, and her character is rooted in good conduct. ||17||

## **The Results of the Moon in the Sixth House**

> If the Moon is situated in the enemy house <note>The sixth house in Vedic astrology is associated with enemies, debts, and diseases</note>, it makes the woman possess little wealth, many enemies, and a lack of humility. She will have a fickle nature, wounds or scars across her body, and will be constantly subjected to the anger of her husband. ||18||

**Meaning**—The woman at whose birth the Moon is positioned in the sixth house will have meager finances and many adversaries. She lacks modesty, possesses a restless temperament, has marks or scars on her limbs, and lives in a state of constant discord with her husband. ||18||

---

## **The Results of the Moon in the Seventh House**

> If the Moon is situated in the house of setting <note>The seventh house, representing marriage and partnerships</note>, it makes the woman clever, beloved by her husband, and endowed with a sense of righteousness and discrimination. She will possess beautiful speech, great wealth, splendor, a devotion to merit, and truthfulness. ||19||

**Meaning**—The woman at whose birth the Moon is positioned in the seventh house will be skillful and dear to her husband. She is endowed with virtue and wisdom, speaks with an excellent and refined voice, possesses great riches and brilliance, and is a devoted, truthful wife dedicated to performing good deeds. ||19||

---

## **The Results of the Moon in the Eighth House**

> If the Moon is in the eighth house, it makes the woman cruel, with poor eyesight, and malformed breasts and private parts. She will be devoid of fine clothing and ornaments, afflicted by disease, and constantly engaged in strange or censurable acts. ||20||

**Meaning**—The woman at whose birth the Moon is positioned in the eighth house <note>The eighth house is traditionally associated with longevity, secrets, and hardships</note> will be heartless and have defective vision. Her physical form and anatomy are described as unattractive; she lacks jewelry and fine dress, suffers from illness, and is perpetually involved in remarkably disgraceful or criticized behaviors. ||20||

## Now, the results of the Moon situated in the Ninth House

> The Moon <note>original: "Shitakara," literally 'the cool-rayed one'</note> residing in the house of Dharma <note>the ninth house, governing religion and fortune</note> produces a woman of great piety and cleverness. She is exceedingly fortunate, highly praiseworthy, and beautiful; she possesses excellent servants and children, and enjoys abundant happiness. ||21||

**Meaning—**The woman at whose time of birth the Moon is situated in the ninth house is very religious, clever among women, fortunate, and worthy of praise. She is beautiful, understands the minds of others, and attains great happiness through excellent servants and sons. ||21||

---

## Now, the results of the Moon situated in the Tenth House

> The Moon residing in the house of Karma <note>the tenth house, governing actions and career</note> produces a woman with abundant gold and wealth, who is well-known. Such a woman is free from worldly desires, the chief of her entire lineage, endowed with a spirit of sacrifice, devoted to merit, and deeply truthful. ||22||

**Meaning—**The woman in whose tenth house the Moon is situated becomes famous for possessing much gold and wealth. She is content (desireless), the most prominent member of her family, charitable, intent on performing meritorious deeds, and is a faithful wife <note>original: "pativrata," a woman devoted to her husband</note>. ||22||

---

## Now, the results of the Moon situated in the Eleventh House

> The Moon residing in the house of Gains <note>the eleventh house</note> makes a woman prosperous, auspicious, and knowledgeable in rituals, as well as a great giver. She is cheerful, filled with affection, inclined toward charity, and possesses a body free from disease. ||23||

**Meaning—**The woman at whose time of birth the Moon is situated in the eleventh house is wealthy, radiant, and knowledgeable in traditional laws and methods. She is a donor, cheerful, endowed with humility, charitable, and possesses a body free from any illness. ||23||

### The Results of the Moon Situated in the House of Expenditure

**The Moon, when placed in the twelfth house, makes a woman**
**prone to excessive spending, stripped of her influence,**
**and of a sharp or harsh temperament. She is wretched, submissive,**
**devoid of moral conduct, lacking in patience, and always afflicted by ailments. ||24||**

Meaning—The woman at whose time of birth the Moon is situated in the twelfth house <note>The twelfth house, or Vyaya-bhava, traditionally governs loss, expenditure, and the subconscious.</note> is a spendthrift whose personal influence has vanished. She possesses a fierce nature, is miserable, lacks ethical guidance, is impatient, and is constantly troubled by disease. ||24||

### The Results of Mars Situated in the Ascendant

**The son of the Earth <note>original: "Bhutanayah" - a common epithet for the planet Mars</note>, when occupying the Ascendant, produces**
**a woman whose body is greatly distressed by blood-related disorders. She is**
**stripped of influence, rejected by her husband,**
**most unfortunate, and filled with excessive pride. ||25||**

Meaning—If Mars is in the Ascendant <note>The first house or Lagna, representing the self and physical body.</note> at the time of a woman's birth, that woman will have a body suffering from many blood-related illnesses. Her influence is lost, she is abandoned by her husband, possesses ill fortune, and is characterized by vanity. ||25||

### The Results of Mars Situated in the House of Wealth

**The son of the Earth, when residing in the house of wealth, makes**
**a woman devoid of great riches and of poor character.**
**She is dominated by others, devoted to lust, sickly,**
**burdened by excessive strife, and lacking in hair. ||26||**

Meaning—The woman at whose time of birth Mars is situated in the second house <note>The Dhana-bhava, governing accumulated wealth and speech.</note> is deprived of great wealth and is of a wicked nature. She is subject to the influence of co-wives <note>The original Hindi "सौत भाव" suggests the presence of or rivalry with a co-wife.</note>, is addicted to sensual pleasures, is prone to illness, and suffers from great hardships and a lack of hair. ||26||

<term>चंद्रफल (Chandra-phala): The astrological results or fruits of the Moon's position.</term>

<term>ज्योतिष (Jyotish): Vedic astrology.</term>
<term>मंगल (Mangal): The planet Mars.</term>

### Now, the results of Mars situated in the Third House
> Mars <note>original: "tritiyasasthaḥ"; literally "situated in the third"</note> situated in the third house produces excellent sons, and makes a woman exceedingly fortunate, of good character, beloved by her kinsmen, devoted to holy men, praiseworthy, free from disease, and of celebrated influence. || 27 ||

**Meaning—**The woman in whose birth chart Mars is located in the third house <term>Third House: traditionally associated with siblings, courage, and efforts</term> is constantly blessed with great fortune, is dear to her brothers, devoted to the company of saints, radiant, healthy, and appropriately influential. || 27 ||

### Now, the results of Mars situated in the Fourth House
> The son of the Earth <note>original: "bhūtanayaḥ"; a common epithet for the planet Mars</note> moving in the fourth house produces a woman who is hopeless, a performer of wicked deeds, bereft of happiness, without wealth, of bad character, rejected by people, and constantly full of anger. || 28 ||

**Meaning—**The woman in whose birth chart Mars is located in the fourth house <term>Fourth House: represents the home, mother, and domestic happiness</term> is one whose hopes are destroyed; she performs censurable acts, lacks happiness, possesses a wicked nature, is abandoned by her people, and remains a constant embodiment of wrath. || 28 ||

### Now, the results of Mars situated in the Fifth House
> The son of the Earth residing in the house of progeny <note>original: "sutāśrito"; referring to the fifth house</note> produces a woman with wicked sons, devoid of compassion, of evil counsel, addicted to sinful practices, bereft of sacred knowledge <note>original: "śrutena hīnāṃ"; lacking knowledge of the Vedas</note>, and one who has destroyed her circle of kinsmen. || 29 ||

**Meaning—**The woman in whose birth chart Mars is located in the fifth house <term>Fifth House: governs children, intelligence, and past merits</term> is a woman with wicked sons, lacking in mercy, a giver of bad advice, devoted to sinful deeds, and devoid of Vedic rites; she either destroys her kin or is herself abandoned by her circle of relatives. || 29 ||

## The Results of Mars in the Sixth House

> The son of the Earth <note>original: "bhutanayah" - an epithet for Mars</note> situated in the house of enemies <note>the 6th house</note> produces a woman who is accompanied by her husband, has destroyed the faction of her enemies, possesses abundant hair, is devoted to good people, is highly educated, and is free from disease. ||30||

**Meaning—**The woman in whose birth chart Mars is located in the sixth house is a woman who possesses a husband, has destroyed the ranks of her enemies, has a great deal of hair, is dedicated to virtuous people, possesses superior knowledge, and has a body free from illness. ||30||

## The Results of Mars in the Seventh House

## The Results of Mars in the Eighth House

**Meaning—**The woman in whose birth chart Mars is located in the eighth house is a woman who suffers from many illnesses, has a weak body, is without a husband, is poor and a victim of sorrows, is filled with grief, commits acts of violence, and is lacking in beauty. ||32||

### Now, the results of Mars situated in the tenth house

> <note>original: "कर्म्माश्रितो भूतनयः प्रसूते नारीं कुकर्म्म श्रवणां कुभावाम् । शीलेन हीनां निरतां विधर्म्मा लज्जाविहीनां मतिवर्जितां च ॥३४॥"</note>

**Meaning—** The woman at whose birth Mars is situated in the **tenth house** <note>The house of profession and action</note> is one who listens to evil deeds, possesses a wicked nature, and is devoid of modesty. She is devoted to false religions, lacks shame, and is bereft of intelligence. ||34||

### Now, the results of Mars situated in the eleventh house

> <note>original: "लाभाश्रितस्संकुरुते महीजः प्रभूतलाभां वनितां निरीहाम् । शुभस्वभावां विविधोपचाराम्बारतां प्रीतिपरां च धर्मे ॥३५॥"</note>

**Meaning—** The woman at whose birth Mars is situated in the **eleventh house** <note>The house of gains/income</note> is a woman of great profit and few desires. She is of good character, performs various acts of service <term>upachara: traditional hospitality or medicinal service</term>, is devoted to her mother, and takes great pleasure in her religious duties. ||35||

### Now, the results of Mars situated in the twelfth house

> <note>original: "व्ययस्थितो भूतनयः प्रसूते नारीं कृतघ्नां गुणवर्जिताङ्गीम् । असद्व्र्वयां पानपरां नृशंसां सदातुरां प्रीतिविवर्जितां च ॥३६॥"</note>

**Meaning—** The woman at whose birth Mars is situated in the **twelfth house** <note>The house of expenditure and loss</note> is ungrateful and lacks virtuous qualities. She spends money on wicked things, is addicted to drinking, is cruel, is always afflicted by illness, and is devoid of affection. ||36||

### The Results of Jupiter in the First House

> If the Teacher of the Gods <note>original: "Devaguru," an epithet for Jupiter</note> resides in the Ascendant at birth, she will be truthful, endowed with beautiful enjoyments, possessed of profound speech, a supporter of the virtuous, having a lovely form, and the most excellent among women.

Meaning—The woman at whose birth <term>Jupiter</term> <note>The planet of wisdom and expansion</note> is situated in the <term>Ascendant</term> <note>The first house or the sign rising on the eastern horizon at birth</note> is devoted to her husband, possesses a refined mind, enjoys life's pleasures, speaks with gravity, loves the company of holy men, has a beautiful body, and is considered the best among women.॥49॥

### The Results of Jupiter in the Second House

> Jupiter situated in the House of Wealth produces a woman with abundant riches, great fortune, and a charming mind; she is righteous, devoted to ethics, a leader, free from envy, and adorned with gold ornaments.

Meaning—The woman at whose birth <term>Jupiter</term> is situated in the second house <note>The house governing wealth and speech</note> possesses great wealth and excellent fortune. She is perceptive, dedicated to moral conduct, a leader whom no one can criticize, and is adorned with gold jewelry.॥50॥

### The Results of Jupiter in the Third House

> The Teacher of the Gods situated in the third house makes a woman whose influence is entirely vanished; she is full of faults, lacking in dignity, and always deprived of wealth.

Meaning—The woman at whose birth <term>Jupiter</term> is situated in the third house <note>The house governing siblings and courage</note> is one whose influence has constantly faded away. She is burdened with many faults, lacks respectability, is deficient in her speech, and is always without wealth.॥51॥

### Now, the results of Jupiter situated in the House of Happiness (Fourth House)

> **The Teacher of the Gods <note>Jupiter/Brihaspati</note> located in the fourth house makes a woman cheerful, endowed with happiness and wealth, possessed of great learning and ornaments, famous, with a body well-honored, and dignified by the weight of her virtues. (52)**

Meaning—The woman at whose birth Jupiter is situated in the House of Happiness <note>the fourth house, governing home and emotions</note> is of a cheerful mind, possesses happiness and wealth, is highly educated, wears many ornaments, and is famous. Her person is worthy of worship due to the dignity of her excellent qualities. (52)

---

### Now, the results of Jupiter situated in the Fifth House

> **The Teacher of the Gods situated in the House of Progeny <note>the fifth house</note> produces a woman with excellent sons, one who has destroyed sinful deeds, is always well-disposed, expert in vows and religious duties, truthful in soul, and grand in beautiful assemblies.**

Meaning—If Jupiter is situated in the fifth house at the time of a woman's birth, that woman will have excellent sons and will have destroyed her sinful actions. She remains of a consistent temperament at all times, is clever in religious vows and duties, is truthful in spirit, and shines brightly in elegant gatherings. (53)

---

### Now, the results of Jupiter situated in the Sixth House

> **Jupiter situated in the House of Enemies <note>the sixth house</note> brings a woman many enemies, yet she is endowed with policy; her body is filled with many calamities and fears, and she is characterized by great pride and the arrows of anger. (54)**

Meaning—The woman at whose birth Jupiter is situated in the sixth house possesses many enemies. Though she is humble <note>or follows policy/ethics</note>, her body is subject to many disasters and terrors. She is one who achieves her own ends and is prone to discharging the "arrows of anger." (54)

---

### Now, the results of Jupiter situated in the Seventh House

> **The Teacher of the Gods gone to the House of the Spouse <note>the seventh house</note> produces a woman of good character and great merit, loved by the people, a scholar of many scriptures, dear to her husband, and endowed with fame.**

## Results of Venus Situated in the Second House

> If Venus is placed in the house of wealth <note>the second house</note>, it produces a woman who is wealthy, possessed of clever movements, and beautiful. She is a standard-bearer of righteousness, devoted to dharma, highly fortunate, famous for her deeds, and soft-spoken.

**Meaning—**The woman in whose birth chart Venus is situated in the second house becomes wealthy, possesses clever gestures, and is beautiful. She is a leader among the religiously minded, devoted to her duties, and is considered blessed. Such a woman is famous for her virtuous actions and speaks with a sweet voice. (62)

## Results of Venus Situated in the Third House

> The teacher of the demons <note>an epithet for Venus/Shukra</note> situated in the third house produces a woman of good deeds, endowed with humility, and joined by many excellent brothers as well as excellent sisters. (63)

**Meaning—**The woman in whose birth chart Venus is situated in the third house is endowed with the best of actions and humility. She is accompanied by many excellent brothers as well as excellent sisters. (63)

## Results of Venus Situated in the Fourth House

> The teacher of the demons situated in the fourth house produces a woman of abundant happiness and great wealth. She is of a playful nature, performs the duties of others, has conquered her senses, and is an ornament to her lineage.

**Meaning—**The woman in whose birth chart Venus is situated in the fourth house possesses great happiness and wealth. Her nature is inclined toward elegance and playfulness; she performs charitable acts for others, has self-control over her senses, and is like a jewel or ornament to her family line. (64)

## Results of Venus Situated in the Fifth House

> Indeed, situated in the fifth house, he makes a woman virtuous, prosperous, and wealthy with many daughters. She is of pleasing appearance and truly detached from worldly attachments...

> Venus situated in the house of fortune produces a woman devoted to righteousness, among the foremost of women in the world; wealthy in various types of garments, shelters, and food; possessing a well-nourished mind and acting with the boldness of a man. (69)

Meaning—The woman at whose birth <term>Venus</term> <note>original: "Shukra"</note> is situated in the <term>Ninth House</term> <note>The house of fortune, religion, and destiny.</note> becomes a leader among the women of the world. She is endowed with many kinds of clothes, dwellings, and food; she possesses a strong and healthy mind and is as generous and capable as a man. (69)

### The Results of Venus Situated in the Tenth House

> The teacher of the demons situated in the house of action produces a woman of renown, accompanied by great wealth; her body is honored for her famous deeds, she is exceedingly beautiful, wise, and deeply truthful.

Meaning—The woman at whose birth <term>Venus</term> <note>referred to here by the epithet "Daitya-guru" or the Preceptor of the Titans</note> sits in the <term>Tenth House</term> <note>The house of career, social status, and public action.</note> becomes famous and possesses excellent wealth. She performs celebrated deeds through her labor, has a body worthy of worship, is highly intelligent and praiseworthy in her form, and is a most faithful wife. (70)

### The Results of Venus Situated in the Eleventh House

> The teacher of the demons situated in the house of gain always produces a woman of abundant profit; she is free from faults, devoted to many scriptures, possesses great influence, and owns various houses.

Meaning—The woman at whose birth <term>Venus</term> is situated in the <term>Eleventh House</term> <note>The house of gains, income, and fulfillment of desires.</note> is always blessed with great profits. She is free from all defects, dedicated to the study of many <term>Shastras</term> <note>Sacred scriptures or systematic treatises.</note>, possesses great influence, and owns multiple residences. (71)

### Now, the results of Venus situated in the Twelfth House

The daughter of Bhrigu <note>original: "Bhruguja"; an epithet for Venus</note> residing in the house of expenditure <note>the 12th House</note> produces a woman who experiences the sorrow of improper spending. She is excessively prone to anger, attached to artificial or deceitful speech, afflicted by diseases, and is a wicked woman devoid of wisdom. ||72||

**Meaning—** The woman in whose birth chart Venus is situated in the twelfth house spends her wealth on unworthy causes. She speaks fabricated words, suffers from illnesses, lacks intelligence, and is "wicked"—meaning she may seek the company of men other than her husband. ||72||

### Now, the results of Saturn situated in the Ascendant

Truly, Saturn <note>original: "Saurah"; the son of the Sun</note> situated in the Ascendant <note>the 1st House or "Lagna"</note> makes a woman possess an utterly deformed body. She suffers from an excess of mucus <note>original: "ama"; undigested toxins or phlegm</note>, lacks any fame, and possesses thick bones and teeth, as well as poor eyesight. ||73||

**Meaning—** The woman in whose birth chart Saturn is situated in the birth ascendant constantly possesses an unattractive body. She suffers from chronic dysentery or digestive ailments, is devoid of glory, has thick bones and teeth, and is blind or has weak vision. ||73||

### Now, the results of Saturn situated in the Second House

The son of the Sun <note>original: "Suryasutah"; Saturn</note> residing in the house of wealth <note>the 2nd House</note> produces a woman devoid of riches and rejected by others. She is always wandering, lacks affection, possesses a cruel nature, and is overwhelmed by diseases. ||74||

**Meaning—** The woman in whose birth chart Saturn is situated in the second house is poor and treated with contempt by people. She lacks humility, possesses a fearless (shameless) disposition, and is afflicted by illness. ||74||

## The Results of Saturn in the Third House

> The son of the Sun <note>original: "Ravija," a name for Saturn</note> situated in the third house produces a woman who is clever, a leader among women, and very fortunate. She possesses many children, is devoted to providing protection, and is always praised by virtuous people. ||75||

Meaning—The woman at whose birth Saturn is located in the third house is the most skillful among clever women and is blessed. Accompanied by many offspring and ready to provide protection, she is constantly praised by holy and noble men. ||75||

## The Results of Saturn in the Fourth House

> Saturn <note>original: "Manda," meaning the slow-moving one</note> located in the house of happiness <note>the 4th house</note> makes a woman possess little joy, lacking in intelligence, and ungrateful. She is of a fickle nature, destitute of wealth, always harmful, and associates with the low-born. ||76||

Meaning—The woman at whose birth Saturn is located in the fourth house is lacking in intellect and fails to acknowledge favors done for her. Her nature is unstable, she is devoid of riches, she always acts with ill intent, and she keeps the company of base men. ||76||

## The Results of Saturn in the Fifth House

> The son of the Sun <note>original: "Bhaskaraja," another epithet for Saturn</note> residing in the house of progeny <note>the 5th house</note> produces a woman without sons and devoid of compassion. She is full of great pride, imitates the behavior of courtesans, and is excluded from the company of the virtuous. ||77||

Meaning—The woman at whose birth Saturn is located in the fifth house is devoid of pity and possesses great arrogance. Her conduct is like that of a prostitute <term>Ganika: a professional courtesan or dancing girl</term>, she lacks the company of the holy, and she remains childless. ||77||

### Now, the results of Saturn situated in the Sixth House

> Saturn <note>original: "mando"</note> situated in the house of enemies <note>the sixth house</note> makes a woman energetic <note>literally "without slowness"</note>, a leader among women, endowed with sons, possessed of abundant clothes and ornaments, devoted to virtues, and beloved by her husband. ||78||

Meaning—The woman at whose time of birth Saturn <note>original: "shanaishvara"</note> is situated in the sixth house is a leader among women, free from lethargy, accompanied by sons, possessing many garments and jewels, intent on good qualities, and dear to her husband. ||78||

> Saturn <note>original: "sauro"</note> situated in the house of setting <note>the seventh house, representing marriage</note> produces a widow, or one always deserted by her husband; she suffers from many diseases, is addicted to drinking, has wicked friends, possesses many faults, and partakes in many sins.

Meaning—The woman at whose time of birth Saturn is situated in the seventh house becomes a widow or remains forever separated from her husband. She is highly diseased, devoted to drinking intoxicants, possesses bad friends, has many vices, and is a sharer in numerous sins. ||79||

> The son of the Sun <note>a title for Saturn</note> in the eighth house produces a woman with an oily <note>or lustrous</note> body who suffers from the faults of her own actions. She is of a wicked nature, has lost her sense of duty and truth, is of a filthy disposition, and is intent on deceiving others. ||80||

Meaning—The woman at whose time of birth Saturn is situated in the eighth house has a greasy or smooth body, is burdened by the faults of her misdeeds, and possesses a wicked nature. She is one who has destroyed her own good karma and truthfulness; she is of a foul nature and is intent on slandering others. ||80||

### Now, the results of Saturn situated in the Ninth House

> The son of the Sun <note>Saturn</note> resting in the house of Dharma <note>the ninth house</note> always produces a woman who is attached to evil deeds.

# The Results of Rahu Situated in the First House (Ascendant)

->As stated by the astrologer Shyam<-

If Rahu is situated in the first house <note>The Ascendant or 'Lagna'</note>, it makes a woman's body misshapen and her character poor. She suffers from many diseases, lacks dignity in her form, is filled with anger, and is rejected by all people. ||85||

**Meaning—** The woman at whose birth Rahu is located in the first house will have an unattractive body, lack modesty, suffer from excessive illness, and possess a body devoid of honor. She remains angry and is treated with contempt by all men. ||85||

# The Results of Rahu Situated in the Second House

If Rahu's position is in the second house, it makes a woman devoid of wealth and a "bad wife" <note>original: 'kukanta'</note>. She is deprived of happiness, becomes a widow, suffers from disease, and is a partaker of sins associated with the misery of poverty. ||86||

**Meaning—** The woman at whose birth Rahu is located in the second house is a woman without wealth and of poor character. She is devoid of comforts, a widow, sickly, and suffers the sins accompanying the sorrows of poverty. ||86||

# The Results of Rahu Situated in the Third House

Rahu <note>original: 'Tamas', meaning darkness or the shadow planet</note> in the third house produces a woman who is deprived of kinsmen and sisters. However, she possesses a well-nourished body, conquers her enemies, is endowed with forgiveness, and is free from disease. ||87||

**Meaning—** The woman at whose birth Rahu is located in the third house is...

# Female Horoscopy

The woman is devoid of brothers and lacks sisters; she possesses a strong body and has conquered the ranks of her enemies. She is endowed with patience and remains free from disease. ||87||

->Now, the results of Rahu situated in the Fourth House<-

> Rahu in the house of happiness <note>The fourth house represents the home, mother, and happiness.</note> makes a woman possess little wealth, abandoned by people, and ungrateful. She has an affection for four-footed animals <note>Specifically livestock or riding animals like horses.</note>, a body prone to illness, and is utterly deprived of maternal happiness. ||88||

**Meaning—**The woman in whose birth chart Rahu is situated in the fourth house is a lady of meager wealth. She is isolated from people and ungrateful. She loves four-footed animals (conveyances, etc.), has a diseased body, and is constantly deprived of the happiness provided by a mother. ||88||

->Now, the results of Rahu situated in the Fifth House<-

> If the Darkness <note>original: "Tamo" (Darkness), a common epithet for Rahu.</note> is in the house called "offspring" <note>The fifth house governs children and intelligence.</note>, it makes a woman intoxicated with pride and devoid of power. She has thick teeth and a large mouth, resembles a courtesan, lacks luster, and is abandoned by her own kin. ||89||

**Meaning—**The woman in whose birth chart Rahu is situated in the fifth house is intoxicated (arrogant), lacks prosperity, and has thick teeth and a large mouth. She resembles a prostitute <note>original: "Ganika," a professional entertainer or courtesan.</note>, lacks radiance, and is forsaken by her own people, such as relatives and children. ||89||

->Now, the results of Rahu situated in the Sixth House<-

> Rahu in the house of enemies <note>The sixth house governs enemies, debts, and health.</note> makes a woman resolute, compassionate, and a conqueror of all enemy factions. She is highly educated, endowed with wealth and grain, always speaks pleasantly, and is the beloved of her husband. ||90||

**Meaning—**The woman in whose birth chart Rahu is situated in the sixth house is resolute and compassionate. She has conquered all enemy forces, possesses great knowledge, is endowed with wealth and grain, always speaks sweetly, and is dear to her husband.

### Now, the results of Rahu situated in the Seventh House

**The Shadow Planet <note>original: "Tamah" - referring to Rahu, the ascending lunar node</note> in the house of the spouse produces a woman devoid of a husband's presence and makes her unsightly. She is of a very wicked mind, miserly, ungrateful, and is always rejected by her own kin. 91.**

Meaning—The woman in whose birth chart Rahu is situated in the seventh house <note>the house governing marriage and partnerships</note> becomes husbandless and unattractive. She is evil-minded, stingy, and ungrateful, and is always abandoned by her circle of relatives. 91.

### Now, the results of Rahu situated in the Eighth House

**When the enemy of the Lord of Day <note>original: "Dinanath-shatru" - Rahu, who "swallows" the Sun during eclipses</note> is in the eighth house, he makes the woman diseased in body, a widow, and unsightly. She is hard-hearted, inclined toward misconduct, and afflicted by great ailments, abandoned by people. 92.**

Meaning—The woman in whose birth chart Rahu is situated in the eighth house <note>the house of longevity and hidden things</note> possesses a body plagued by disease. She takes the form of a widow, has a harsh heart, a character inclined toward adultery, is tormented by major illnesses, and is shunned by society. 92.

### Now, the results of Rahu situated in the Ninth House

**When the enemy of the Lord of Night <note>original: "Rajnish-shatru" - Rahu, the enemy of the Moon</note> is in the house of penance <note>the ninth house of religion and fortune</note>, he makes the woman irreligious and a supporter of foreign faiths. She is fond of meat and drink, cruel, wanders aimlessly, and is devoid of fame. 93.**

Meaning—The woman in whose birth chart Rahu is situated in the ninth house is devoid of her own religion and supports the religions of others. She is fond of meat, devoted to intoxicants <note>original: "madya" - wine or liquor</note>, fearless in cruelty, wanders without purpose, and is without any glory or reputation. 93.

### Now, the results of Rahu situated in the Tenth House

> If Rahu is situated in the tenth house at birth, it produces a woman who is bereft of father and mother. Rejected by her husband and at odds with her own kin, she is filled with anger and has completely destroyed the side of her enemies. ||94||

**Meaning—** The woman in whose birth chart Rahu is located in the tenth house <note>The house of career, social status, and public life</note> becomes one who is deprived of her mother and father. > Rahu in the eleventh house makes a woman exceedingly beautiful, always humble, and beloved by her husband. Accompanied by horses and elephants, she is joyful and endowed with excellent servants and sons. ||95||

**Meaning—** The woman in whose birth chart Rahu is located in the eleventh house <note>The house of gains and aspirations</note> is endowed with beauty, is always characterized by humility, and is dear to her husband. ### Now begins the Chapter on Birth in the Mula Nakshatra

->Regarding this, Narada describes the characteristics of the "Abhukta Mula" <note>A particularly inauspicious period during the transition between certain lunar mansions</note>—<-

> The child born in the interval of one watch between Jyeshtha and Mula, or between Ashlesha and Magha, is born in Abhukta Mula. ||1||

**Meaning—** A boy or girl born during the one *prahar* <note>A traditional unit of time lasting approximately three hours</note> that bridges the gap between the Jyeshtha and Mula constellations, or the one *prahar* between the Ashlesha and Magha constellations, is said to be born in *Abhukta Mula*. Specifically, this "one watch" consists of the final 3.75 *ghatis* <note>A ghati is 24 minutes; 3.75 ghatis is 90 minutes</note> of Jyeshtha and the first 3.75 *ghatis* of Mula. Similarly, the final 3.75 *ghatis* of Ashlesha and the first 3.75 *ghatis* of Magha are also called *Abhukta Mula*. However, one must certainly verify the exact calculation of the half-watch according to mathematical astronomy.

<summary>This page concludes the discussion on Rahu's influence in the twelfth house for women and introduces the concept of Abhukta Mula, a critical astronomical junction considered spiritually significant or challenging for newborns.</summary>
<keywords>Rahu, Nakshatra, Jyeshtha, Mula, Ashlesha, Magha, Abhukta Mula, Prahar, Astrology, Commentary, Vashistha, Garga, Varahamihira</keywords>

--- Page 190 ---
<meta>This page continues the discussion on female horoscopy, specifically focusing on the dangerous astrological period known as Abhukta-mula.</meta>

<page-num>166</page-num>
-># Female Horoscopy<-

One should first determine the full extent of the <term>Nakshatra</term> <note>Lunar mansion; the 27 divisions of the sky through which the moon passes</note>. Dividing that Nakshatra into sixteen parts, the junction between the end of one and the beginning of the next is called <term>Abhukta-mula</term> <note>Literally "un-enjoyed root"; a particularly inauspicious transitional period between certain stars</note>. ||1||

### Regarding the timing of Abhukta-mula, Vashistha says:

> The interval between the stars of the Serpent, Indra, and Pushan, and the stars that immediately follow them, is the measure of one <term>Prahar</term> <note>A period of approximately three hours</note>. One should always abandon a daughter born during this Abhukta-mula period. ||2||
<note>original: "भुजंगपौरंदरपौष्णभानां..." - The Serpent refers to Ashlesha, Indra to Jyeshtha, and Pushan to Revati.</note>

**Meaning—**The interval between the stars Ashlesha, Jyeshtha, and Revati and those that follow them—namely the junctions of Ashlesha-Magha, Jyeshtha-Mula, and Revati-Ashwini—is called Abhukta-mula, lasting for one watch of the day. A girl born during this watch should be permanently abandoned. ||2||

### Likewise, Shaunaka describes the definition of Abhukta-mula:

> The interval between the Serpent and Ancestor stars, the Indra and Root stars, and the Pushan and Horseman stars is the measure of one watch. A father should not even look upon a daughter born during this Abhukta-mula. ||3||
<note>original: "सार्पचपैत्र्यंत्वथशाक्रमूल..." - These are poetic names for the Nakshatras: Ashlesha-Magha, Jyeshtha-Mula, and Revati-Ashwini.</note>

**Meaning—**The gap between Ashlesha-Magha, Jyeshtha-Mula, and Revati-Ashwini is the measure of the Abhukta watch. A father must not look at a daughter born in that watch. ||3||

### Abandonment of a child born in Abhukta-mula:

> One should abandon a son, and even a daughter, born in Abhukta-mula.

**Meaning—**A boy or a girl born during the Abhukta-mula period should be cast out.

### Now, the remedy if abandonment is not possible:

> Alternatively, the father should not look upon the child's face for eight years. ||4||

**Meaning—**If one does not have the strength to abandon the child, then after eight years have passed and <term>Shanti</term> <note>A pacification ritual or propitiatory ceremony to offset bad omens</note> has been performed, the father may look upon the child's face. ||4||

<summary>This page defines the inauspicious astrological period of Abhukta-mula occurring at specific lunar junctions and prescribes the abandonment of children born then, or an eight-year separation and ritual purification if abandonment is impossible.</summary>
<keywords>Abhukta-mula, Nakshatra, Ashlesha, Jyeshtha, Revati, Vashistha, Shaunaka, Shanti ritual, female horoscopy, child abandonment</keywords>

### Now, the results of being born in Mula according to the quarters

If born in the first quarter of Mula, there is the destruction of the father; in the second, the mother; in the third, the destruction of wealth and grain; and in the fourth, there is an arrival of wealth. ||5||

**Meaning—** If a child is born in the first quarter <term>charana; one of four parts of a lunar mansion</term> of the Mula <note>Mula is a lunar mansion associated with roots and sometimes perceived as inauspicious</note> constellation, it causes the destruction of the father. Born in the second quarter, it causes the destruction of the mother. Born in the third quarter, it causes the destruction of wealth and grain. Born in the fourth quarter, it brings about the arrival of wealth. ||5||

### Now, the results of being born in Ashlesha according to the quarters

The results for the Ashlesha constellation are the same, but in reverse order starting from the last quarter. ||6||

**Meaning—** If a girl or boy is born in the first quarter of the Ashlesha <note>Ashlesha is the "Clinging Star," associated with serpents</note> constellation, there is an arrival of wealth. Born in the second quarter, it causes the destruction of wealth and grain. Born in the third quarter, it causes the destruction of the mother. And born in the fourth quarter, it causes the destruction of the father. ||6||

### Now, the results for a female birth in the quarters of Mula

In the first quarter of Mula, suffering to livestock occurs. Born in the second quarter, she becomes the giver of all happiness. ||7||
In the third foot of Mula, she is the destroyer of the father's side of the family. Born in the fourth foot, the woman causes the ruin of the mother's side of the family. ||8||

**Meaning—** A girl who is born in the first quarter of Mula causes suffering to the domestic animals. Born in the second quarter, she provides complete happiness. ||7|| Born in the third quarter, she destroys the father's side of the family <term>pitripaksha; the paternal lineage</term>. Born in the fourth quarter, she causes the ruin of the mother's side of the family <term>matripaksha; the maternal lineage</term>. ||8||

### Now, Narada speaks on the results of being born in Mula and Ashlesha

A son or a daughter born in Mula certainly kills the father-in-law.

<vocab>Astrology, Constellation (Nakshatra), Mula, Ashlesha, Quarter (Charana), Result (Phala), Narada, Paternal side, Maternal side</vocab>

<header>Female Horoscopy</header>

**Meaning—** If a boy or a girl is definitely born under the *Mula* or *Ashlesha* <term>Nakshatras</term> <note>Lunar mansions or star clusters in Vedic astrology</note>, then she causes the destruction of her father-in-law.

### Now, the Exception to This:

**The last quarter of that (Mula) and the first quarter of Ashlesha do not cause such harm. ||9||**

**Meaning—** If a boy or girl is born in the final quarter of the *Mula* constellation, or in the first quarter of the *Ashlesha* constellation, then the "Mula-born defect" does not apply; rather, it is auspicious for the father-in-law. ||9||

### Now, the Combinations for the Destruction of the Father-in-law and Others:

**One born in Mula destroys the father-in-law; one born in Vyala (Ashlesha) destroys his wife (the mother-in-law). One born in Aindri (Jyeshtha) destroys the husband's elder brother; one born in Dwidaiva (Vishakha) destroys the husband's younger brother. ||10||**

**Meaning—** A girl born in the *Mula* constellation causes the destruction of her father-in-law. A girl born in the *Ashlesha* constellation causes the destruction of her mother-in-law. A girl born in the *Jyeshtha* constellation causes the destruction of her husband's elder brother. A girl born in the *Vishakha* constellation causes the destruction of her husband's younger brother <term>Devar</term>. ||10||

### Likewise, the result of being born in Mula—according to Shripati:

**A girl born in the constellations of Mula or Ahi (Ashlesha) destroys the mother and father of her husband. Those born in the final quarter of Dwisha (Vishakha) are wicked/harmful, as is one born in the final quarter of Jyeshtha. ||11||**

**Meaning—** A girl born in the *Mula* constellation causes the destruction of her husband's mother and father. Furthermore, a girl or boy born in the final quarter of the *Vishakha* constellation gives evil results to the husband's parents. In the same way, a girl born in the final quarter of the *Jyeshtha* constellation gives the aforementioned evil results. ||11||

<header>With Hindi Commentary</header>

And so, the result of being born in the constellations of Mula and Ashlesha—according to the sage Ganapati:

> A daughter or son born under the name of Ashlesha destroys the mother-in-law. Those born under Mula destroy the father-in-law. A girl born under Jyeshtha destroys her husband's elder brother. ||12||

**Meaning**—A girl or a boy born in the Ashlesha <note>The 9th Nakshatra, associated with serpents</note> constellation causes the destruction of the mother-in-law. Those born in the Mula <note>The 19th Nakshatra, located at the galactic center</note> constellation cause the destruction of the father-in-law. A girl born in the Jyeshtha constellation causes the destruction of the husband's elder brother, and a boy born then causes the destruction of the elder sister-in-law <note>specifically the wife's elder sister</note>. ||12||

->Exceptions to this rule<-

> The first quarter of Ashlesha and the final quarter of Mula, as well as the first three quarters of Vishakha and Jyeshtha, are auspicious. ||13||

**Meaning**—A child born in the first quarter <term>Pada</term><note>A Nakshatra is divided into four quarters or Padas</note> of the Ashlesha constellation, the final quarter of the Mula constellation, and the beginning quarters of Vishakha and Jyeshtha constellations is auspicious. The remaining three quarters of these constellations are considered unfavorable. ||13||

### Now, the three types of Gandanta <note>Inauspicious "knot" points or transitions</note> are described—by Shripati:

> The intervals between Revati and Ashvini, Ashlesha and Magha, and Jyeshtha and Mula—these pairs are known as Gandanta. These four-Ghati <note>A Ghati is a unit of time equal to 24 minutes; four Ghatis equal 96 minutes</note> periods are unfavorable for travel, birth, and marriage. ||14||

**Meaning**—The junctions between the constellations of Revati-Ashvini, Ashlesha-Magha, and Jyeshtha-Mula are called Gandanta. Specifically, the four Ghatis at the end of one and the four Ghatis at the start of the next are considered unfavorable. They yield negative results for travel, the time of birth, marriage, and the sacred thread ceremony. The last 4 Ghatis of Revati, Ashlesha, and Jyeshtha, and the first 4 Ghatis of Ashvini, Magha, and Mula are unfavorable. ||14||

### Now, the Gandanta of the Lunar Days is described—by Narada:

> The junction of the lunar days known as Purna and Ananda <note>Referring to the transition between the 5th, 10th, or 15th Tithi and the start of the next cycle</note> for a duration of two Ghatis...

<sig>7</sig>

<meta>This page continues the discussion on astrological timing and its effects on life events, specifically focusing on the 'Gandanta' or transitional periods.</meta>

<page-num>170</page-num>
<header>The Female Horoscope <note>original: "Stri-Jatakam"</note></header>

->**The Gandanta transition brings death at birth, during journeys, weddings, and sacred vows. ||15||**<-

**Meaning—** The transitional periods <note>Gandanta: a spiritual or temporal 'knot' or danger zone in the lunar calendar</note> of the following lunar dates—the Full Moon, the 1st, 5th, 6th, 10th, and 11th days—are each two *ghatis* <term>ghati: a traditional unit of time equal to 24 minutes</term> long. Specifically, the final *ghati* of the 15th, 5th, and 10th days, and the initial *ghati* of the 1st, 6th, and 11th days are called Gandanta. These periods bring death; births, journeys, marriages, and the sacred thread ceremony <term>Yajnopavita: the initiation ritual for young students</term> are forbidden during these times. ||15||

### Now, the Gandanta of the Ascendant Signs

->**Between Cancer and Leo, Scorpio and Sagittarius, and Pisces and Aries,**<-
->**the interval is the Gandanta; lasting half a ghati, it bestows death. ||16||**<-

**Meaning—** The junction between the signs of Cancer–Leo, Scorpio–Sagittarius, and Pisces–Aries is called the Ascendant Gandanta <note>Lagna-Gandanta</note>, lasting half a *ghati* (12 minutes). Specifically, the final 15 *pals* <term>pal: a unit of time equal to 24 seconds</term> of the 4th, 8th, and 12th signs, and the first 15 *pals* of the 5th, 9th, and 1st signs are known as Gandanta. Performing journeys, marriages, births, or sacred thread ceremonies during these moments leads to death. ||16||

### Now, the timing of Gandanta effects

->**Born in the day, it destroys the father; born at night, the mother.**<-
->**Born at twilight, it destroys the self; there is no health in Gandanta. ||17||**<-

**Meaning—** If a child is born during the day in a Gandanta period, it causes the destruction of the father. If born during the night in Gandanta, it causes the destruction of the mother. If born during the twilight junctions of day and night, the child causes its own destruction. A child born in Gandanta is never free from affliction. ||17||

### Now, the duration of the Gandanta defect—according to Yavana <note>Yavana: likely referring to the 'Yavanajataka' or Greek-influenced astrological tradition</note>

->**It kills the father within a year, and the mother within three years.**<-
->**The self is destroyed within one month; thus is Gandanta remembered by the wise. ||18||**<-

Meaning—A child born during the <term>Gandanta</term> <note>a "knot" or critical junction point in the zodiac between water and fire signs, considered highly inauspicious</note> period destroys the father within one year, destroys the mother within three years, and destroys his own soul within one month; so the learned scholars have declared. ||18||

### Now, regarding the abandonment of those born in Gandanta

> The abandonment of all those born in Gandanta is ordained.

Meaning—It is the prescribed rule that a child born during any of the aforementioned Gandanta periods should be abandoned. <note>In historical astrological texts, "abandonment" often referred to symbolic separation or placing the child in the care of others until remedial rites were performed.</note>

### Now, the duration of separation if abandonment is not possible

> One should avoid seeing the child until he becomes six months old. ||19||

Meaning—If a child is born during the Gandanta period, looking upon him is forbidden until he reaches the age of six months. ||19||

### Now, the exceptions for those born in Gandanta

> If born in Mula, Ashlesha, or Revati, and the Lord of the Ascendant does not see the Moon; and if the Moon is weak, joined with malefic planets, and devoid of the aspect of benefic planets; then the sages declare there is no fault for those born in such a Gandanta. ||20||

Meaning—If a child is born under the <term>Nakshatras</term> <note>lunar mansions or star clusters</note> of Mula, Ashlesha, or Revati, and the <term>Lagna</term> <note>the rising sign or Ascendant</note> lord does not aspect the Moon, and the Moon is weak, influenced by "cruel" planets, and lacks the gaze of auspicious planets, then the child born in that Gandanta is considered faultless; so the great sages have said. ||20||

### Another exception to the Gandanta fault

> If the first quarter of Mula occurs during the night, then there is no destruction of the father. If the second quarter occurs during the day, then there is not even the slightest fault for the mother. ||21||

# Female Horoscopy
<note>original: "Stri-Jatakam"</note>

Meaning—If a child is born during the first quarter of the *Mula* constellation <note>The 19th lunar mansion, often considered inauspicious</note> at night, that child does not cause the destruction of the father. Similarly, if a child is born during the second quarter of the *Mula* constellation during the day, that child does not bring harm to the mother. ||21||

### And so says Pitamaha:
<note>Pitamaha is a traditional authority in Vedic astrology</note>

->The defects of the junction points of constellations and lunar days do not apply if the Moon is powerful.<-
->Likewise, the defect of the junction point of the ascendant does not apply if Jupiter is endowed with strength.<- ||22||

Meaning—If a child is born during the *Gandanta* <note>A "knot" or spiritual junction point between zodiac signs/constellations considered volatile</note> of a constellation or a lunar day, but the Moon is strong in the birth chart, consider it harmless. If a child is born during the *Gandanta* of the Ascendant but Jupiter is strong, it should be known as harmless. ||22||

### Now, another remedy—Vasistha:
<note>Vasistha is one of the most revered Vedic sages</note>

->The auspicious time known as the Abhijit Muhurta destroys all the defects of the Gandanta.<-
->Just as a hunter kills a deer or a flock of birds, so does Abhijit destroy all Gandanta defects.<- ||23||

Meaning—If a child is born with the complete *Gandanta* defect, but the time of birth falls within the *Abhijit Muhurta* <note>An auspicious midday period lasting about 48 minutes</note>, then just as a hunter destroys a group of deer or birds, the *Abhijit* destroys all the *Gandanta* defects. ||23||

### Now, the consideration of the Mula Tree—Narapati:

->The Root <gloss>7</gloss>, the Trunk <gloss>8</gloss>, the Bark <gloss>10</gloss>, the Branches <gloss>11</gloss>, the Leaves <gloss>12</gloss>, the Flowers <gloss>5</gloss>, the Fruit <gloss>4</gloss>, the Crest <gloss>3</gloss>.<-
->The Sages (7), the Vasus (8), the Directions (10), the Rudras (11), the Suns (12), the Five, the Oceans (4), the Fires (3).<- ||24||
<note>The second line uses symbolic numerical words to correspond to the first line's values</note>

Meaning—For a child born in the *Mula* constellation, one should consider the "Tree of Mula." By imagining the form of a tree, one distributes the 60 *Ghatikas* <note>A traditional unit of time equal to 24 minutes</note> of the constellation's duration as follows: 7 in the root, 8 in the trunk, 10 in the bark, 11 in the branches, 12 in the leaves, 5 in the flowers, 4 in the fruit, and 3 at the head (top) of the tree. In this manner, one should analyze the effects of the 60 *Ghatikas* of the constellation. ||24||

### Now, the results are stated—from the Japanava:

> If birth occurs in the root, there is destruction of the root; in the trunk, the destruction of the lineage.
> In the bark, there is distress for the mother; in the branches, for everyone. ||25||
> In the leaves, one should know there is kingship; in the flower, the position of a minister is remembered.
> In the fruit, there is abundant wealth; in the crest, a short life. ||26||

**Meaning—** If at the time of a child's birth, the <term>Ghati</term><note>A unit of time equal to 24 minutes; a Nakshatra is divided into 60 Ghatis.</note> of the <term>Nakshatra</term><note>Lunar mansion or star sign.</note> falls in the **root**, that child destroys the "root" (the family foundation). If it falls in the **trunk**, it destroys the lineage. If it falls in the **bark**, it causes distress to the mother. If it falls in the **branches**, it brings all kinds of happiness <note>The Hindi commentary interprets "akhilasya" (of all) as universal happiness/prosperity here, though the Sanskrit verse can imply general distress.</note>. ||25|| And if it falls in the **leaf**, it gives the result of kingship. If it falls in the **flower**, it makes one a minister. If it falls in the **fruit**, one attains great wealth. If it falls in the **crest** (top), it makes one short-lived. One should speak after considering these results of the 60 Ghatis of the Mula Nakshatra. One must consider specifically those Ghatis in which the birth occurs. ||26||

### Now, the placement of the Mula-Chakra at birth—from Pitamaha:

> Five Ghatis placed in the head, one becomes a king.
> Seven in the mouth, death of the parents. Four in the shoulders, great strength. ||27||
> Eight in the arms, strength. Three in the throat, one possesses a palace.
> Nine in the heart, a minister to the king. Two in the navel, one knows strength. ||28||
> Ten in the private parts, one is highly lustful. Six in the knees, great intellect.
> Six in the feet, death. This was spoken by the Self-Existent One (Brahma). ||29||

**Meaning—** At the time of birth (for girls or boys), one should envision a human form and place five Ghatis in the **tuft/head**; a boy or girl born in those Ghatis becomes a king or queen. Seven Ghatis in the **mouth** bring death to the father. Four Ghatis in the **shoulders** make one very powerful. ||27|| Know that eight Ghatis in the **arms** grant strength...

In the ghatis of the heart, it brings the attainment of a kingdom (6). In the ghatis of the navel, it causes a short lifespan (7). In the ghatis of the waist, it brings wonderful happiness (8). ||35|| Born in the ghatis of the thighs...

### Now, the knowledge of the residence of Mula according to the months

> In Margashirsha, Phalguna, Vaishakha, and Jyeshtha, Mula resides in the underworld.
> In Shravana, Kartika, Chaitra, and Pausha, Mula resides on the earth. ||38||
> In Ashadha, Ashvina, Bhadrapada, and Magha, Mula is situated in the heavens.

Meaning—In the months of Margashirsha, Phalguna, Vaishakha, and Jyeshtha, the Mula nakshatra resides in the underworld <term>Patala Loka: the subterranean realm</term>. In the months of Shravana, Kartika, Chaitra, and Pausha, Mula resides in the world of the mortals <term>Mrityu Loka: the Earthly realm</term>. ||38|| In the months of Ashadha, Ashvina, Bhadrapada, and Magha, Mula resides in heaven.

### Now, the results of this residence are told

> When Mula is in heaven, a kingdom is gained; in the underworld, there is the arrival of wealth.
> When Mula is in the world of mortals, then one should point out obstacles. ||39||

**Thus ends the fifteenth chapter, titled "Description of Birth in the Unlucky Mula Nakshatra," in the *Stri Jataka* (Female Horoscopy) composed by the Royal Astrologer Pandit Shyamlal, son of Shri Baldev Prasad, the jewel of the Gauda lineage residing in the excellent Vansh-Bareilly.**

<vocab>Ashlesha Nakshatra, Mula Nakshatra, Underworld (Rasatala), Earth (Bhutala), Heaven (Divi/Svarga), World of Mortals (Mrityu Loka), Birth in Unlucky Mula (Abhuktamula-janma), Female Horoscopy (Stri Jataka), Shyamlal</vocab>

<summary>This page details the specific astrological consequences of being born during different time-segments of the Ashlesha and Mula stars, and explains how the "location" of the Mula star changes by month, affecting the subject's fortune.</summary>
<keywords>Ashlesha nakshatra, Mula nakshatra, female astrology, Hindu calendar months, Patala, Mrityu Loka, Shyamlal, Stri Jataka, royal astrology</keywords>

<page-num>177</page-num>
<header>With Hindi Commentary</header>

Meaning—If a child is born in the *Mula* <note>The 19th lunar mansion, often associated with roots or foundations</note> constellation and the residence of *Mula* is in the Heavens, it bestows kingship. If the residence of *Mula* is in the Underworld, it brings the arrival of wealth. However, if the residence of *Mula* is in the Mortal Realm (Earth), then obstacles and disruptions should be predicted. ॥39॥

# Now, the Chapter on the Peace Rites for Mula Birth is Described

->Regarding this, Vashistha <note>A revered Vedic sage and author of astrological treatises</note> states the three timings for the peace rite:<-

**According to the methods prescribed in the scriptures, truly at the end of the birth-impurity period,**
**or in the third month, or at the end of the year. ॥1॥**

Meaning—The *Mula Shanti* <note>A ritual to pacify the perceived negative influences of being born under the Mula constellation</note> for a child born in the *Mula* constellation should be performed according to the scriptural methods at the end of the *Sutaka* <note>The period of ritual impurity following a birth, usually lasting 10 to 40 days</note>, or in the third month, or it is proper to do it at the end of the year. ॥1॥

->Garga <note>Another ancient sage and authority on Indian astronomy/astrology</note> states another time for the peace rite:<-

**When a son is born in the Mother-Ganda, the wise one, at the end of the birth-impurity,**
**Should perform the peace rite on that same constellation to remove that defect. ॥2॥**

Meaning—If a boy or girl is born in the *Matri-Ganda* <note>A specific inauspicious astrological junction related to the mother's side</note>, their peace rite should be performed once the birth-impurity is over. Alternatively, to remove the *Ganda* defect <note>Ganda-mula refers to specific points in the zodiac considered spiritually precarious</note>, it is proper to perform the peace rite on the return of the same constellation in which the child was born. ॥2॥

<vocab>Mula Nakshatra (Lunar Mansion), Shanti (Peace Rite), Jyotish (Astrology), Sutaka (Birth Impurity), Gandadosha (Astrological Defect), Vashistha, Garga</vocab>

<summary>This page concludes the discussion on the effects of the Mula constellation based on its cosmic residence and introduces the ritual timings for performing remedial peace rites (Shanti) as prescribed by sages Vashistha and Garga.</summary>
<keywords>Mula Nakshatra, Shanti rituals, birth impurity, Sutaka, Gandadosha, Vedic astrology, Vashistha, Garga, Shyamsundari commentary</keywords>
